```json
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "pricolici-c01",
      "source_id": "pricolici-s1",
      "quote": "A Pricolici (pronounced /pri.koˈlit͡ʃʲ/) (same form in plural) is a Romanian vampire with werewolf-like attributes, transformed from a child breastfed after its mother has weaned it risks.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pricolici (bentuk jamaknya sama) adalah vampir Rumania bercirikan manusia serigala, yang berasal dari anak yang disusui lagi setelah disapih.",
        "en": "A pricolici (same form in the plural) is a Romanian vampire with werewolf traits, arising from a child breastfed again after weaning."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pricolici-c02",
      "source_id": "pricolici-s1",
      "quote": "Pricolici are similar to a vârcolac, although the latter sometimes symbolises a goblin, whereas the pricolici always has wolf-like characteristics, they have the ability to transform into ordinary people or animals.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pricolici mirip vârcolac, tetapi selalu bercirikan serigala dan dapat berubah menjadi manusia biasa atau hewan.",
        "en": "Pricolici resemble the vârcolac, but always have wolf traits and can turn into ordinary people or animals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pricolici-c03",
      "source_id": "pricolici-s1",
      "quote": "While a strigoi possesses anthropomorphic qualities similar to the ones it had before death, a pricolici always resembles a wolf or dog. Malicious, violent men are often said to become pricolici after death, in order to continue harming other humans.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Berbeda dari strigoi yang tetap mirip manusia, pricolici selalu mirip serigala atau anjing; lelaki jahat dan kejam sering dikatakan menjadi pricolici setelah mati untuk terus mencelakai manusia.",
        "en": "Unlike the strigoi, which keeps a human look, the pricolici always resembles a wolf or dog; wicked, violent men are often said to become pricolici after death to keep harming people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pricolici-c04",
      "source_id": "pricolici-s1",
      "quote": "Some Romanian folklore delineates that Pricolici are werewolves in life and after they die, return as vampires.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian cerita rakyat Rumania menyebut pricolici adalah manusia serigala semasa hidup dan kembali sebagai vampir setelah mati.",
        "en": "Some Romanian folklore holds that pricolici are werewolves in life and return as vampires after death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pricolici-c05",
      "source_id": "pricolici-s1",
      "quote": "Even as recently as modern times, many people living in rural areas of Romania have claimed to have been viciously attacked by abnormally large and fierce wolves. Apparently, these wolves attack silently, unexpectedly and only solitary targets.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bahkan di zaman modern banyak warga pedesaan Rumania mengaku diserang serigala yang luar biasa besar dan buas, yang menyerang diam-diam, tiba-tiba, dan hanya orang yang sendirian.",
        "en": "Even in modern times many rural Romanians have claimed to be attacked by unusually large, fierce wolves that strike silently, suddenly and only lone victims."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pricolici-c06",
      "source_id": "pricolici-s1",
      "quote": "The etymology of the word is unknown; although it probably has Latin origins from the word \"Lycus\" i.e \"wolf\" (prico+lici).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Asal katanya tidak diketahui, tetapi mungkin dari bahasa Latin lycus, \"serigala\".",
        "en": "The word's origin is unknown, though it may come from Latin lycus, 'wolf'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pricolici-c07",
      "source_id": "pricolici-s2",
      "quote": "Algunos folclores rumano describen a los Pricolici como hombres lobo en vida que después de morir, regresan como vampiros. Esto también da lugar a la leyenda de los vampiros que pueden transformarse en animales como lobos, perros, búhos o murciélagos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Spanyol juga menyebut mereka manusia serigala semasa hidup yang kembali sebagai vampir, asal legenda vampir yang dapat menjadi serigala, anjing, burung hantu, atau kelelawar.",
        "en": "The Spanish article also calls them werewolves in life who return as vampires, the root of the legend of vampires turning into wolves, dogs, owls or bats."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pricolici-c08",
      "source_id": "pricolici-s3",
      "quote": "Приколичами называли: мертвецов, вставших из могилы в образе волка или собаки, реже других диких животных; живых людей, способных на время превращаться в волков или собак",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Rusia, pricolici adalah sebutan untuk orang mati yang bangkit dari kubur berwujud serigala atau anjing, maupun orang hidup yang untuk sementara dapat berubah menjadi serigala atau anjing.",
        "en": "According to the Russian article, pricolici were the dead risen from the grave as wolves or dogs, and also living people able to turn into wolves or dogs for a time."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Quinotaur adalah \"banteng bertanduk lima\" dari laut dalam Kronik Fredegar abad ke-7, yang dianggap ayah Meroveus dan leluhur raja-raja Merovingia.",
    "en": "The Quinotaur is the five-horned sea bull of the 7th-century Chronicle of Fredegar, held to be the father of Meroveus and ancestor of the Merovingian kings.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "quinotaur-c01",
      "quinotaur-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Quinotaur, dari bahasa Latin \"banteng bertanduk lima\", adalah makhluk laut yang disebut Kronik Fredegar abad ke-7 sebagai binatang Neptunus. Ia dianggap menjadi ayah Meroveus dengan menyerang istri raja Frank Chlodio, menurut versi Italia saat sang ratu mandi di laut, sehingga menurunkan dinasti Merovingia.",
      "en": "The Quinotaur, Latin for 'bull with five horns', is a sea creature called a beast of Neptune in the 7th-century Chronicle of Fredegar. It is said to be the father of Meroveus, having assaulted King Chlodio's wife, in the Italian version as she bathed in the sea, and so the founder of the Merovingian line.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "quinotaur-c01",
        "quinotaur-c04",
        "quinotaur-c05",
        "quinotaur-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pseudo-Fredegar menyamakannya dengan Neptunus dan Minotaurus; tidak jelas apakah ciri laut dan banteng itu asli atau tambahan penulis Kristen, dan apakah namanya terjemahan makhluk Frank atau ciptaan para rohaniwan.",
      "en": "Pseudo-Fredegar likened it to Neptune and the Minotaur; it is unclear whether the sea and bull traits are original or added by Christian writers, and whether the name translates a Frankish creature or was coined by clerics.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "quinotaur-c02",
        "quinotaur-c03"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "Quinotaurus",
    "language": "Latin",
    "literal_meaning": {
      "id": "banteng bertanduk lima",
      "en": "bull with five horns"
    },
    "claim_ids": [
      "quinotaur-c04"
    ]
  },
  "story_mode": {
    "who": {
      "id": "Banteng laut bertanduk lima.",
      "en": "A five-horned sea bull.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "quinotaur-c01",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "quinotaur-c01",
      "source_id": "quinotaur-s1",
      "quote": "The Quinotaur (Latin: Quinotaurus) is a mythical sea creature mentioned in the 7th century Frankish Chronicle of Fredegar. Referred to as \"the beast of Neptune which resembles a Quinotaur\", it was held to have fathered Meroveus by attacking the wife of the Frankish king Chlodio and thus to have sired the line of Merovingian kings.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Quinotaur (Latin: Quinotaurus) adalah makhluk laut mitis yang disebut dalam Kronik Fredegar abad ke-7 sebagai \"binatang Neptunus yang menyerupai Quinotaur\", yang dianggap menjadi ayah Meroveus dengan menyerang istri raja Frank Chlodio sehingga menurunkan raja-raja Merovingia.",
        "en": "The Quinotaur (Latin Quinotaurus) is a mythical sea creature in the 7th-century Chronicle of Fredegar, 'the beast of Neptune which resembles a Quinotaur', held to have fathered Meroveus by attacking the wife of the Frankish king Chlodio and so sired the Merovingian kings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "quinotaur-c02",
      "source_id": "quinotaur-s1",
      "quote": "The \"bull with five horns\" was likened by Pseudo-Fredegar (interpolating Gregory of Tours, who authored an earlier record of the legend) to both Neptune and the Minotaur, as it was both seaborne and taurine.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "\"Banteng bertanduk lima\" ini oleh Pseudo-Fredegar, yang menambahkan catatan Gregorius dari Tours, disamakan dengan Neptunus dan Minotaurus karena berasal dari laut dan berwujud banteng.",
        "en": "The 'bull with five horns' was likened by Pseudo-Fredegar, interpolating Gregory of Tours, to both Neptune and the Minotaur, being both seaborne and bull-like."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "quinotaur-c03",
      "source_id": "quinotaur-s1",
      "quote": "It is not known whether one or both traits are original to the legend or if their combination is an accretion by one or both of the Christian authors. The clerical Latinity of the name does not indicate whether it is a translation of some genuine Frankish creature or a coining.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tidak diketahui apakah ciri laut dan banteng itu asli dari legenda atau tambahan penulis Kristen, dan nama Latinnya tidak menunjukkan apakah ia terjemahan makhluk Frank asli atau ciptaan baru.",
        "en": "It is unknown whether the sea and bull traits are original or added by the Christian authors, and the clerical Latin name does not show whether it translates a genuine Frankish creature or is a coinage."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "quinotaur-c04",
      "source_id": "quinotaur-s2",
      "quote": "Le Quinotaure (Lat. Quinotaurus, « taureau à cinq cornes ») est une créature marine légendaire mentionnée dans la Chronique de Frédégaire du VIIe siècle.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, nama Quinotaurus berarti \"banteng bertanduk lima\".",
        "en": "According to the French article, the name Quinotaurus means 'bull with five horns'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "quinotaur-c05",
      "source_id": "quinotaur-s2",
      "quote": "Il est considéré comme le père de Mérovée (et donc à l'origine de la lignée des rois mérovingiens) à la suite du viol de la reine Altina, épouse du roi franc Clodion le Chevelu.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dianggap ayah Mérovée setelah memerkosa Ratu Altina, istri raja Frank Clodion si Berambut Panjang.",
        "en": "It is considered the father of Merovech after raping Queen Altina, wife of the Frankish king Chlodio the Long-Haired."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "quinotaur-c06",
      "source_id": "quinotaur-s3",
      "quote": "Esso è ritenuto essere il padre di Meroveo, in quanto si unì alla moglie del re franco Clodione mentre ella stava facendo il bagno in mare",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Italia menyebut makhluk ini bersetubuh dengan istri raja Clodione ketika ia sedang mandi di laut.",
        "en": "The Italian article says it coupled with King Chlodio's wife while she was bathing in the sea."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
    "en": "Ratoncito Pérez is the little mouse of Spanish and Hispanic American tradition who comes at night to swap a child's milk tooth under the pillow for money or a gift.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Ratoncito Pérez atau Ratón Pérez adalah tokoh masa kecil dalam budaya Spanyol dan Amerika Hispanik: anak yang tanggal gigi susunya menaruhnya di bawah bantal, dan tikus kecil ini datang saat mereka tidur untuk menggantinya dengan uang atau hadiah, seperti Peri Gigi. Di sebagian Meksiko, Peru, dan Chili ia disebut Ratón de los Dientes.",
      "en": "Ratoncito Pérez or Ratón Pérez is a childhood figure of Spanish and Hispanic American culture: a child puts a lost milk tooth under the pillow, and the little mouse comes during sleep to replace it with money or a gift, like the Tooth Fairy. In parts of Mexico, Peru and Chile he is the Ratón de los Dientes.",
      "claim_ids": [

    {
      "id": "Tikus bernama Ratón Pérez muncul dalam dongeng lisan, dan pada 1894 Luis Coloma menjadikannya pengumpul gigi dalam cerita pesanan Ratu Maria Christina untuk Alfonso XIII, dengan Pérez tinggal di kotak biskuit toko kue Prast di Madrid.       "en": "A mouse called Ratón Pérez appeared in oral tales, and in 1894 Luis Coloma made him a tooth collector in a tale Queen Maria Christina commissioned for Alfonso XIII, with Pérez living in a biscuit box at the Prast confectionery in Madrid.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "ratoncito-perez-c01",
      "source_id": "ratoncito-perez-s1",
      "quote": "El Ratoncito Pérez or Ratón Pérez ... Perez the Little Mouse or Perez Mouse) is a fantasy figure of early childhood in Spanish and Hispanic American cultures.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ratoncito Pérez atau Ratón Pérez (\"Tikus Kecil Pérez\") adalah tokoh khayalan masa kanak-kanak dalam budaya Spanyol dan Amerika Hispanik.",
        "en": "El Ratoncito Pérez or Ratón Pérez ('Pérez the Little Mouse') is a fantasy figure of early childhood in Spanish and Hispanic American cultures."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ratoncito-perez-c02",
      "source_id": "ratoncito-perez-s1",
      "quote": "The folklore states that when children lose one of their milk teeth, they should place it underneath their pillow or on their bedside table and he will visit while they sleep, replacing the lost tooth with a small payment or gift, as does the Tooth Fairy in other cultures.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut tradisi, anak yang tanggal gigi susunya menaruhnya di bawah bantal atau di meja samping tempat tidur, lalu Pérez datang saat mereka tidur dan menggantinya dengan uang atau hadiah kecil, seperti Peri Gigi di budaya lain.",
        "en": "By tradition, children who lose a milk tooth put it under the pillow or on the bedside table, and he comes while they sleep and replaces it with a small payment or gift, like the Tooth Fairy elsewhere."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ratoncito-perez-c03",
      "source_id": "ratoncito-perez-s1",
      "quote": "Although he first appeared in oral tradition folktales such as The Vain Little Mouse, it was Luis Coloma who in 1894 turned him into a tooth dealer in a tale written for an eight-year-old King Alfonso XIII.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia pertama muncul dalam dongeng lisan seperti El ratoncito vanidoso, tetapi Luis Coloma-lah yang pada 1894 menjadikannya pengumpul gigi dalam cerita untuk Raja Alfonso XIII yang berusia delapan tahun.",
        "en": "He first appeared in oral folktales such as The Vain Little Mouse, but it was Luis Coloma who in 1894 made him a tooth collector in a tale written for the eight-year-old King Alfonso XIII."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ratoncito-perez-c04",
      "source_id": "ratoncito-perez-s1",
      "quote": "In 1894, Queen Maria Christina commissioned Coloma to write a tale for King Alfonso XIII, who had just lost a tooth at the age of eight. Coloma's story follows Ratón Pérez, who lived with his family in a box of cookies at the basement of Prast confectionery store in Madrid",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ratu Maria Christina memesan cerita itu untuk Alfonso XIII yang baru tanggal giginya; dalam cerita Coloma, Ratón Pérez tinggal bersama keluarganya di kotak biskuit di ruang bawah tanah toko kue Prast di Madrid.",
        "en": "Queen Maria Christina commissioned the tale for Alfonso XIII, who had just lost a tooth; in Coloma's story Ratón Pérez lives with his family in a biscuit box in the basement of the Prast confectionery in Madrid."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ratoncito-perez-c05",
      "source_id": "ratoncito-perez-s1",
      "quote": "He is generally known as \"El Ratoncito Pérez\", except in some regions of Mexico, Guatemala, Peru and Chile, where he is called \"El Ratón de los Dientes\" ... The Tooth Mouse), and in Argentina, Venezuela, Uruguay and Colombia, where he is simply known as \"El Ratón Pérez\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia umumnya disebut El Ratoncito Pérez, kecuali di sebagian Meksiko, Guatemala, Peru, dan Chili, tempat ia disebut El Ratón de los Dientes (Tikus Gigi), dan di Argentina, Venezuela, Uruguay, dan Kolombia, tempat ia disebut El Ratón Pérez.",
        "en": "He is usually called El Ratoncito Pérez, except in parts of Mexico, Guatemala, Peru and Chile, where he is El Ratón de los Dientes (the Tooth Mouse), and in Argentina, Venezuela, Uruguay and Colombia, where he is El Ratón Pérez."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ratoncito-perez-c06",
      "source_id": "ratoncito-perez-s2",
      "quote": "El origen más probable del ratoncito y su enlace con un hada proviene de un cuento francés del siglo XVIII de la baronesa d'Aulnoy: La Bonne Petite Souris (El buen ratoncito).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, asal yang paling mungkin adalah dongeng Prancis abad ke-18 karya Baroness d'Aulnoy, La Bonne Petite Souris.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, the most likely origin is the 18th-century French tale by Baroness d'Aulnoy, La Bonne Petite Souris."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ratoncito-perez-c07",
      "source_id": "ratoncito-perez-s2",
      "quote": "En otros lugares hay otros seres fantásticos encargados de recoger los dientes, como Tooth Fairy («Hada de los dientes») en los países germanos, l'Angelet («el Angelito») o La rateta  («la Ratita») en Cataluña, Maritxu teilatukoa («Mari la del tejado») en el País Vasco",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di tempat lain ada makhluk pengumpul gigi lain, seperti Tooth Fairy di negara Jermanik, l'Angelet atau La rateta di Katalonia, dan Maritxu teilatukoa di negeri Basque.",
        "en": "Elsewhere other beings collect teeth, such as the Tooth Fairy in Germanic countries, l'Angelet or La rateta in Catalonia and Maritxu teilatukoa in the Basque Country."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ratoncito-perez-c08",
      "source_id": "ratoncito-perez-s1",
      "quote": "El Ratoncito Pérez stars in the 2006 Spanish-Argentine film The Hairy Tooth Fairy and its 2008 sequel.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ratoncito Pérez dibintangi film Spanyol-Argentina The Hairy Tooth Fairy (2006) dan sekuelnya pada 2008.",
        "en": "El Ratoncito Pérez stars in the 2006 Spanish-Argentine film The Hairy Tooth Fairy and its 2008 sequel."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ratoncito-perez-c09",
      "source_id": "ratoncito-perez-s3",
      "quote": "По традиции дарит ребёнку подарок вместо выпавшего молочного зуба, положенного под подушку.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Rusia juga menyebut ia memberi hadiah sebagai ganti gigi susu yang ditaruh di bawah bantal.",
        "en": "The Russian article also says he gives a child a present in exchange for a milk tooth placed under the pillow."
      }
    }
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Akbuzat adalah kuda putih bersayap mitologi Turkik, leluhur totem bangsa Bashkir dan Tatar dan kuda pahlawan Ural-Batyr, yang tinggal di langit dan ditambatkan pada Bintang Utara.",
    "en": "Akbuzat is the white winged horse of Turkic mythology, totem ancestor of the Bashkir and Tatar peoples and Ural-Batyr's heroic steed, who lives in the sky tethered to the Pole Star.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "akbuzat-c01",
      "akbuzat-c03",
      "akbuzat-c04",
      "akbuzat-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Akbuzat, \"kuda bersayap yang terang\", adalah kuda pahlawan dan leluhur totem bangsa-bangsa Turkik, lahir dari totemisme dan pemujaan kuda; warna putihnya melambangkan kehidupan dan kematian yang baik. Dalam mitologi Tatar, Akbuzat dan Kukbuzat tinggal di langit, turun membantu para pahlawan, lalu ditambatkan pada Bintang Utara sementara tujuh pencuri kuda, rasi Biduk, mengejar mereka tanpa hasil.",
      "en": "Akbuzat, 'light winged horse', is a heroic horse and totem ancestor of the Turkic peoples, born of totemism and the horse cult; its whiteness stands for a good life and death. In Tatar mythology Akbuzat and Kukbuzat live in the sky, come down to help heroes and are tethered to the Pole Star, while seven horse thieves, the Great Bear, chase them in vain.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "akbuzat-c01",
        "akbuzat-c02",
        "akbuzat-c03",
        "akbuzat-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Bashkir ia leluhur para tulpar dan kuda Ural-Batyr dalam epos Akbuzat; ia tidak terbakar atau tenggelam, menghancurkan gunung dengan kukunya, dan busa mulutnya menyembuhkan luka. Opera Akbuzat dipentaskan pada 1942, dan monumennya berdiri di Burzyansky sejak 2021.",
      "en": "In Bashkir mythology it is ancestor of the tulpars and Ural-Batyr's horse in the epic Akbuzat; it neither burns nor drowns, smashes mountains with its hooves, and its mouth foam heals wounds. The opera Akbuzat was staged in 1942, and its monument has stood in Burzyansky since 2021.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "akbuzat-c05",
        "akbuzat-c06",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "akbuzat-c01",
      "source_id": "akbuzat-s1",
      "quote": "Akbuthat (bachkir : Aҡbuҙat ; tatar : Akbuzat  - cheval ailé léger) est un personnage du folklore et de la mythologie turque, l'ancêtre (ancêtre totem) de ces peuples, et vertu héroïque du cheval.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Akbuzat (Bashkir: Aҡbuҙat; Tatar: Akbuzat, \"kuda bersayap yang terang\") adalah tokoh cerita rakyat dan mitologi Turkik, leluhur totem bangsa-bangsa itu dan kuda pahlawan yang luhur.",
        "en": "Akbuzat (Bashkir Aҡbuҙat; Tatar Akbuzat, 'light winged horse') is a figure of Turkic folklore and mythology, the totem ancestor of those peoples and a virtuous heroic horse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "akbuzat-c02",
      "source_id": "akbuzat-s1",
      "quote": "L'origine d'Akbuzat est liée à la large diffusion du totémisme parmi les peuples turciques, ainsi qu'au culte du cheval, qui jouait un rôle important dans la vie des Turcs. La couleur blanche symbolise une naissance réussie et heureuse, une vie, une mort paisible et un enterrement digne.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Asal-usul Akbuzat terkait luasnya totemisme di kalangan bangsa Turkik dan pemujaan kuda, sedangkan warna putih melambangkan kelahiran yang selamat, hidup bahagia, kematian damai, dan pemakaman yang layak.",
        "en": "Akbuzat's origin is tied to widespread totemism among Turkic peoples and the cult of the horse, while white symbolises a safe birth, a happy life, a peaceful death and a worthy burial."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "akbuzat-c03",
      "source_id": "akbuzat-s1",
      "quote": "La mythologie tatare comprend l'histoire de deux chevaux héroïques divins, Akbuzat et Kukbuzat (cheval bleu ciel), vivant dans le ciel ; ces chevaux participent aux actions des héros, descendant sur terre, puis remontant au ciel, où ils sont attachés à un pieu de fer",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mitologi Tatar mengisahkan dua kuda pahlawan ilahi, Akbuzat dan Kukbuzat (kuda biru langit), yang hidup di langit, turun ke bumi membantu para pahlawan, lalu kembali ke langit dan ditambatkan pada tiang besi.",
        "en": "Tatar mythology tells of two divine heroic horses, Akbuzat and Kukbuzat (the sky-blue horse), who live in the sky, come down to help heroes, then return to the sky, tied to an iron stake."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "akbuzat-c04",
      "source_id": "akbuzat-s2",
      "quote": "где они привязаны к железному колу (тат. Тимерказык — Полярная звезда) и пасутся вокруг него. Семь конокрадов (Большая Медведица) преследуют Акбузата и Кукбузата, с целью похищения, но догнать не могут.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Rusia, tiang besi itu adalah Timerkazyk (Bintang Utara) tempat mereka merumput, dan tujuh pencuri kuda (Biduk) terus mengejar Akbuzat dan Kukbuzat tetapi tak pernah dapat menyusul.",
        "en": "According to the Russian article, the iron stake is Timerkazyk (the Pole Star) around which they graze, and seven horse thieves (the Great Bear) chase Akbuzat and Kukbuzat but can never catch them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "akbuzat-c05",
      "source_id": "akbuzat-s1",
      "quote": "Dans la mythologie bachkire, c'est un cheval héroïque ailé magique, ancêtre des Tulpars. Cheval d'Oural-Batyr, personnage principal de l'épopée bachkire (kubair) « Akbuzat ».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Bashkir ia kuda pahlawan bersayap yang ajaib, leluhur para tulpar, kuda Ural-Batyr, dan tokoh utama epos Bashkir (kubair) Akbuzat.",
        "en": "In Bashkir mythology it is a magic winged heroic horse, ancestor of the tulpars, Ural-Batyr's horse and the title character of the Bashkir epic (kubair) Akbuzat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "akbuzat-c06",
      "source_id": "akbuzat-s3",
      "quote": "Күктә тыуған, ерҙә һәм һыу аҫтында йәшәй ала, утта янмай, һыуҙа батмай, тояҡтары менән тауҙарҙы емерә һәм диңгеҙҙе айыра.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Bashkir, ia lahir di langit, dapat hidup di darat dan di bawah air, tidak terbakar api, tidak tenggelam, menghancurkan gunung dengan kukunya, dan membelah laut.",
        "en": "According to the Bashkir article, it was born in the sky, can live on land and under water, does not burn in fire or sink in water, smashes mountains with its hooves and parts the sea."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "akbuzat-c07",
      "source_id": "akbuzat-s3",
      "quote": "Аҡбуҙаттың ауыҙ күбеге шифалы — уны һөртөп, яраны бөтәштерәләр.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Busa mulut Akbuzat berkhasiat menyembuhkan luka bila dioleskan.",
        "en": "Akbuzat's mouth foam heals wounds when rubbed on them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "akbuzat-c08",
      "source_id": "akbuzat-s1",
      "quote": "Antonio Emmanuilovich Spadavecchia a écrit avec Kh. Zaimov l'opéra « Akbuzat » (« Le cheval magique »), créé pour la première fois en 1942 au Théâtre académique d'opéra et de ballet de l'État bachkir",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Antonio Spadavecchia bersama Kh. Zaimov menulis opera Akbuzat (\"Kuda Ajaib\") yang pertama dipentaskan pada 1942 di Teater Opera dan Balet Negara Bashkir.",
        "en": "Antonio Spadavecchia with Kh. Zaimov wrote the opera Akbuzat ('The Magic Horse'), first staged in 1942 at the Bashkir State Opera and Ballet Theatre."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "akbuzat-c09",
      "source_id": "akbuzat-s1",
      "quote": "En 2021, dans le district de Burzyansky, près du lac Yylkysykkan, un monument de cinq mètres de haut (le cheval lui-même y mesure 3,5 mètres) a été coulé à la fonderie de fer Kasl.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2021 di distrik Burzyansky dekat danau Yylkysykkan didirikan monumen setinggi lima meter (kudanya sendiri 3,5 meter) yang dicor di pengecoran Kasl.",
        "en": "In 2021 a five-metre monument (the horse itself 3.5 metres) cast at the Kasli foundry was set up in the Burzyansky district near Lake Yylkysykkan."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
    "id": "Bitard adalah hewan khimera legenda Poitiers yang tak pernah tertangkap, sasaran perburuan pura-pura untuk mengerjai pendatang baru dan lambang perkumpulan mahasiswa Poitiers.",
    "en": "The Bitard is a never-caught chimera of Poitiers legend, target of mock hunts to prank newcomers and emblem of a Poitiers student society.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bitard atau Outarde adalah hewan mitis yang konon tinggal di hutan Ligugé dekat Poitiers, khimera berkepala musang, bertubuh ikan, berkaki kelinci atau basset, dan berbulu merak atau kalkun. Perburuannya adalah lelucon: mahasiswa senior membawa mahasiswa baru ke hutan dengan karung dan lampu sampai korban tertangkap penjaga hutan; lelucon serupa tercatat di kalangan pembantu rumah tangga pada 1879.",
      "en": "The Bitard or Outarde is a mythical animal said to live in the forest of Ligugé near Poitiers, a chimera with a marten's head, a fish's body, hare's or basset's legs and peacock or turkey feathers. Hunting it is a prank: senior students lead newcomers into the woods with a sack and a lamp until the victim is caught by a rural guard; a similar prank among servants is recorded in 1879.",

      "en": "Since the 1920s the Bitard has been the emblem of the Ordre du Vénéré Bitard, a University of Poitiers student society that links it to Rabelais because the word bitard appears in Pantagruel.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bitard-c01",
      "source_id": "bitard-s2",
      "quote": "Selon la légende, le Bitard (ou Outarde) serait un animal mythique qui vivrait dans la forêt de Ligugé près de Poitiers. Il a donné son nom à une confrérie étudiante, l'ordre du Vénéré Bitard, inspirée des écrits de Rabelais.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, Bitard (atau Outarde) adalah hewan mitis yang tinggal di hutan Ligugé dekat Poitiers, dan menjadi nama perkumpulan mahasiswa Ordre du Vénéré Bitard yang terilhami tulisan Rabelais.",
        "en": "By legend the Bitard (or Outarde) is a mythical animal living in the forest of Ligugé near Poitiers, which gave its name to a student brotherhood, the Ordre du Vénéré Bitard, inspired by Rabelais."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bitard-c02",
      "source_id": "bitard-s2",
      "quote": "Le Bitard est une créature chimérique, semi-divine, à tête de fouine, corps de carpe, pattes de lièvre, avec des plumes de paon. Il porte autour du cou des palmes académiques.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bitard adalah makhluk khimera setengah dewa berkepala musang, bertubuh ikan mas, berkaki kelinci, berbulu merak, dan berkalung palmes académiques.",
        "en": "The Bitard is a chimeric, semi-divine creature with a marten's head, a carp's body, a hare's legs and peacock feathers, wearing academic palms round its neck."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bitard-c03",
      "source_id": "bitard-s2",
      "quote": "Tel qu'il est décrit à l'origine : « le Bitard tient sa tête de la fouine, son corps du saumon, son derrière empenné de plumes de dindon, ses pattes du basset. »",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gambaran aslinya menyebut kepalanya dari musang, tubuhnya dari ikan salmon, bagian belakangnya berbulu kalkun, dan kakinya dari anjing basset.",
        "en": "As first described, it has a marten's head, a salmon's body, a rump feathered with turkey plumes and a basset hound's legs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bitard-c04",
      "source_id": "bitard-s2",
      "quote": "Le conte originel relate que, parmi les étudiants de Poitiers, des \"anciens\" accompagnent des jeunes dans les bois, à la recherche de cet animal mythique qu'est le bitard. Devant l'attirer dans un sac, avec une lumière, le jeune étudiant se fait surprendre par un garde-champêtre, et doit alors s'acquitter d'une amende pour braconnage...",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah aslinya menceritakan mahasiswa senior di Poitiers membawa mahasiswa baru ke hutan untuk memburu bitard dengan karung dan lampu, lalu mahasiswa baru itu tertangkap penjaga hutan dan harus membayar denda perburuan liar.",
        "en": "The original tale tells how senior Poitiers students take newcomers into the woods to hunt the bitard with a sack and a light, until the newcomer is caught by a rural guard and fined for poaching."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bitard-c05",
      "source_id": "bitard-s2",
      "quote": "On retrouve mention de cette farce hors du monde étudiant, par exemple chez des domestiques, lors de l'accueil de nouveaux, comme relaté dans les Bulletins de la Société de statistique du département des Deux-Sèvres de 1879.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lelucon ini juga dikenal di luar dunia mahasiswa, misalnya di kalangan pembantu rumah tangga saat menyambut pendatang baru, seperti dicatat Bulletins de la Société de statistique du département des Deux-Sèvres tahun 1879.",
        "en": "The prank is also found outside student life, for example among servants welcoming newcomers, as recorded in the 1879 Bulletins of the statistical society of Deux-Sèvres."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bitard-c06",
      "source_id": "bitard-s1",
      "quote": "It is the symbol of the Ordre du Vénéré Bitard (Loué Soit-Il !) or Order of the Venerated Bitard (May He be Blessed!), a student association of University of Poitiers (France) created in the 1920s.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bitard adalah lambang Ordre du Vénéré Bitard, perkumpulan mahasiswa Universitas Poitiers yang didirikan pada 1920-an.",
        "en": "It is the emblem of the Ordre du Vénéré Bitard, a student association of the University of Poitiers founded in the 1920s."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bitard-c07",
      "source_id": "bitard-s1",
      "quote": "The folklore of the Order refers to François Rabelais as the word bitard appears twice in hunting outcomes in Pantagruel.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tradisi perkumpulan itu merujuk François Rabelais karena kata bitard muncul dua kali dalam hasil perburuan dalam Pantagruel.",
        "en": "The Order's lore refers to François Rabelais, since the word bitard appears twice among hunting spoils in Pantagruel."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bitard-c08",
      "source_id": "bitard-s3",
      "quote": "El Bitardo es un animal legendario que los Bitardos veneran.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Spanyol menyebut Bitard hewan legendaris yang dipuja para anggota perkumpulan Bitard.",
        "en": "The Spanish article calls the Bitard a legendary animal venerated by the Bitards."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bitard-c09",
      "source_id": "bitard-s2",
      "quote": "C'est le principe également de la chasse au dahut",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Prinsip yang sama berlaku pada perburuan Dahu (dahut).",
        "en": "The same principle underlies the hunt for the Dahu (dahut)."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia en, fr, dan es; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Buggane adalah raksasa berbulu hitam pengubah wujud dari cerita rakyat Pulau Man, terkenal karena terus merobek atap gereja Santo Trinian.",
    "en": "The buggane is a black-haired shapeshifting ogre of Isle of Man folklore, famous for repeatedly tearing the roof off St Trinian's church.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Buggane adalah makhluk raksasa pemakan manusia dari cerita rakyat Manx, mirip troll Skandinavia. Wujud aslinya berbulu hitam kasar, bermata seperti obor, dan bertaring tajam, tetapi ia dapat menjadi anak sapi hitam atau manusia berkuku kuda. Sebagai makhluk gaib ia tidak dapat menyeberangi air atau menginjak tanah suci, dan kadang dipanggil peri untuk menghukum orang, seperti ibu rumah tangga di Glen Maye.",
      "en": "The buggane is a huge ogre of Manx folklore, akin to the Scandinavian troll. Its natural form has coarse black hair, torch-like eyes and sharp tusks, but it can become a black calf or a horse-hoofed man. As a magical being it cannot cross water or tread holy ground, and fairies sometimes summon it to punish people, like the housewife of Glen Maye.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "buggane-c01",
      "source_id": "buggane-s1",
      "quote": "In Manx folklore, a buggane (or boagane) was a huge ogre-like creature native to the Isle of Man. Some have considered them akin to the Scandinavian troll.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Manx, buggane (atau boagane) adalah makhluk besar mirip raksasa pemakan manusia asli Pulau Man, yang oleh sebagian orang dianggap mirip troll Skandinavia.",
        "en": "In Manx folklore a buggane (or boagane) was a huge ogre-like creature native to the Isle of Man, which some consider akin to the Scandinavian troll."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buggane-c02",
      "source_id": "buggane-s1",
      "quote": "A shapeshifter, the buggane is generally described as a malevolent being that can appear as a large black calf or human with ears or hooves of a horse. It was large enough to tear the roof off a church.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Buggane adalah pengubah wujud yang jahat, dapat muncul sebagai anak sapi hitam besar atau manusia bertelinga atau berkuku kuda, dan cukup besar untuk merobek atap gereja.",
        "en": "A malevolent shapeshifter, the buggane can appear as a large black calf or a human with a horse's ears or hooves, and is big enough to tear the roof off a church."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buggane-c03",
      "source_id": "buggane-s1",
      "quote": "Its natural form is described as \"covered with a mane of coarse, black hair; it had eyes like torches, and glittering sharp tusks\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wujud aslinya \"tertutup surai rambut hitam kasar, bermata seperti obor, dan bertaring tajam berkilau\".",
        "en": "Its natural form is 'covered with a mane of coarse, black hair; it had eyes like torches, and glittering sharp tusks'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buggane-c04",
      "source_id": "buggane-s1",
      "quote": "As magical creatures, bugganes were unable to cross water or stand on hallowed ground.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai makhluk gaib, buggane tidak dapat menyeberangi air atau berdiri di tanah suci.",
        "en": "As magical creatures, bugganes could not cross water or stand on hallowed ground."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buggane-c05",
      "source_id": "buggane-s1",
      "quote": "His plan was interdicted through the intervention of St. Trinian. Invoked by the captain with a promise to build a chapel in his honour, the saint guided the ship safely into Peel Harbour.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rencana buggane yang ingin mengandaskan kapal digagalkan Santo Trinian, yang menuntun kapal dengan selamat ke Pelabuhan Peel setelah sang kapten berjanji membangun kapel untuknya.",
        "en": "The buggane's plan to wreck a ship was foiled by St Trinian, who guided it safely into Peel Harbour after the captain promised to build him a chapel."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buggane-c06",
      "source_id": "buggane-s1",
      "quote": "Incensed, the buggane screamed, \"St. Trinian should never have a whole church in Ellan Vannin.\" When the chapel came to be built, three times the local people put a roof on, and three times the buggane tore it off.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Marah, buggane berteriak bahwa Santo Trinian tidak akan pernah punya gereja utuh di Ellan Vannin; tiga kali warga memasang atap kapel, tiga kali pula buggane merobeknya.",
        "en": "Enraged, the buggane cried that St Trinian should never have a whole church in Ellan Vannin; three times the people roofed the chapel and three times the buggane tore it off."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buggane-c07",
      "source_id": "buggane-s1",
      "quote": "Bugganes were occasionally called upon by the fairies to punish people that had offended them. The buggane of Glen Maye would have pitched a lazy housewife into a waterfall for putting off baking until after sunset, had she not cut loose the strings of her apron to escape.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Buggane kadang dipanggil para peri untuk menghukum orang yang menyinggung mereka; buggane Glen Maye nyaris melempar ibu rumah tangga yang malas ke air terjun karena memanggang setelah matahari terbenam, tetapi perempuan itu lolos dengan memotong tali celemeknya.",
        "en": "The fairies sometimes called on bugganes to punish those who offended them; the Glen Maye buggane would have thrown a lazy housewife into a waterfall for baking after sunset had she not cut her apron strings to escape."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buggane-c08",
      "source_id": "buggane-s2",
      "quote": "Selon des légendes celtes irlandaises, le détroit de Calf, au sud de l'île de Man, fut créé au cours d'un combat entre le géant irlandais Finn Mac Cumaill et un buggane.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, legenda Irlandia menyebut selat Calf di selatan Pulau Man terbentuk dalam pertarungan antara raksasa Irlandia Finn Mac Cumaill dan seekor buggane.",
        "en": "According to the French article, Irish legend says the Calf Sound south of the Isle of Man was formed in a fight between the Irish giant Finn Mac Cumaill and a buggane."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buggane-c09",
      "source_id": "buggane-s3",
      "quote": "Een Buggane was in de Manx mythologie een groot Oger-achtig wezen, afkomstig van het Eiland Man.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Belanda juga menyebut buggane makhluk besar mirip raksasa pemakan manusia dari mitologi Manx.",
        "en": "The Dutch article also calls the buggane a large ogre-like being of Manx mythology."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
    {
      "id": "cuegle-c01",
      "source_id": "cuegle-s1",
      "quote": "The cuegle is a monster in Cantabrian folklore. Walking on two legs and roughly humanoid in shape, it is believed to have black skin, a long beard, grey hair, three arms without hands or fingers, five rows of teeth, a single stubby horn and three eyes in its head: one yellow, one red, and one blue.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cuegle adalah monster cerita rakyat Cantabria yang berjalan dengan dua kaki dan berwujud mirip manusia, berkulit hitam, berjanggut panjang, berambut kelabu, bertangan tiga tanpa telapak atau jari, bergigi lima baris, bertanduk satu yang pendek, dan bermata tiga: kuning, merah, dan biru.",
        "en": "The cuegle is a monster of Cantabrian folklore, two-legged and roughly humanlike, with black skin, a long beard, grey hair, three arms without hands or fingers, five rows of teeth, one stubby horn and three eyes: yellow, red and blue."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuegle-c02",
      "source_id": "cuegle-s1",
      "quote": "It is said to have great strength despite its small size.  The cuegle attacks people and livestock, and is reputed to steal babies from the cradle.  It may be protected against by placing oak or holly leaves, which it finds repulsive, in the cradle.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meski kecil ia sangat kuat, menyerang manusia dan ternak, dan konon mencuri bayi dari buaian; ia dapat ditangkal dengan daun ek atau holly, yang dibencinya, di buaian.",
        "en": "Small as it is, it is very strong, attacks people and livestock and is said to steal babies from the cradle; oak or holly leaves, which repel it, protect the cradle."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuegle-c03",
      "source_id": "cuegle-s2",
      "quote": "tiene la piel negra, una barba larga, pelo gris, tres brazos sin manos o dedos, cinco filas de dientes, un cuerno rechoncho y de uno a tres ojos en su cabeza. Uno verde, uno rojo y uno azul.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, cuegle bermata satu sampai tiga, berwarna hijau, merah, dan biru.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article the cuegle has one to three eyes, green, red and blue."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuegle-c04",
      "source_id": "cuegle-s2",
      "quote": "El Cuegle puede ver cada uno, pasado, presente y futuro. El futuro lo ve con el ojo de la frente, el presente con el derecho y el pasado con el izquierdo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cuegle dapat melihat masa lalu, kini, dan depan: masa depan dengan mata di dahi, masa kini dengan mata kanan, dan masa lalu dengan mata kiri.",
        "en": "The cuegle can see past, present and future: the future with its forehead eye, the present with its right eye and the past with its left."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuegle-c05",
      "source_id": "cuegle-s3",
      "quote": "Els bebès poden ser protegits mitjançant la col·locació de fusta de roure o les fulles de grèvol, que propicien un efecte apotropaic, en el bressol.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Katalan juga menyebut bayi dapat dilindungi dengan kayu ek atau daun holly di buaian.",
        "en": "The Catalan article also says babies can be protected by putting oak wood or holly leaves in the cradle."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuegle-c06",
      "source_id": "cuegle-s3",
      "quote": "En la sèrie de televisió americana Grimm es fa referència a aquest monstre, sent així, originari de la regió càntabra d'Espanya i posseint tres braços i tres ulls.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster ini dirujuk dalam serial televisi Amerika Grimm sebagai makhluk dari Cantabria bertangan tiga dan bermata tiga.",
        "en": "The monster is referenced in the American TV series Grimm as a creature from Cantabria with three arms and three eyes."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Moura encantada adalah gadis gaib tersihir dari cerita rakyat Portugal dan Galisia yang menjaga harta, gua, dan mata air sambil menyisir rambutnya dengan sisir emas, menunggu dibebaskan.",
    "en": "The moura encantada is an enchanted magical maiden of Portuguese and Galician folklore who guards treasures, caves and springs, combing her hair with a golden comb as she waits to be freed.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "enchanted-moura-c01",
      "enchanted-moura-c02",
      "enchanted-moura-c03",
      "enchanted-moura-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Moura encantada adalah gadis gaib yang sangat cantik dari cerita rakyat Portugal dan Galisia, hidup di bawah kutukan seolah tertidur sampai suatu keadaan mematahkannya. Ia sering bernyanyi sambil menyisir rambut emas atau hitamnya dengan sisir emas. Menurut cerita lama, para moura adalah jiwa gadis yang ditinggalkan menjaga harta para mouros encantados, dan mereka menjaga kastel, gua, jembatan, sumur, dan sungai; mereka pengubah wujud dan pembangun batu yang kuat.",
      "en": "The moura encantada is a very beautiful magical maiden of Portuguese and Galician folklore, living under a spell as if asleep until some circumstance breaks it. She often sings while combing her golden or black hair with a golden comb. By old lore the mouras are souls of maidens left guarding the mouros encantados' treasure, and they guard castles, caves, bridges, wells and rivers; they are shapeshifters and mighty stone-builders.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "enchanted-moura-c01",
        "enchanted-moura-c02",
        "enchanted-moura-c03",
        "enchanted-moura-c04",
        "enchanted-moura-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Leite de Vasconcelos menduga mereka menyerap ciri nimfa dan roh alam, Consiglieri Pedroso menyebut mereka jin air perempuan, dan dongengnya dianggap berasal dari tradisi Kelt pra-Romawi. Nama mouro, pasangan laki-lakinya, menurut Isodoro Millán terkait kata Latin mortuus, \"orang mati\".",
      "en": "Leite de Vasconcelos thought they absorbed traits of nymphs and nature spirits, Consiglieri Pedroso called them female water genies, and their tales are thought pre-Roman Celtic. The name of their male counterpart, mouro, is linked by Isodoro Millán to Latin mortuus, 'dead'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "enchanted-moura-c06",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "enchanted-moura-c01",
      "source_id": "enchanted-moura-s1",
      "quote": "The Enchanted moura or moura encantada (enchanted female Mouros) is a supernatural being from the fairy tales of Portuguese and Galician folklore. Very beautiful and seductive, she lives under an imposed occult spell. Shapeshifters, the mouras encantadas occupy liminal spaces and are builders with stone of formidable strength.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Moura encantada adalah makhluk gaib dari dongeng Portugal dan Galisia, sangat cantik dan menggoda, hidup di bawah kutukan gaib; mereka pengubah wujud yang menghuni ruang ambang dan pembangun batu yang sangat kuat.",
        "en": "The moura encantada is a supernatural being of Portuguese and Galician fairy tales, very beautiful and seductive, living under an occult spell; shapeshifters, they haunt liminal places and are stone-builders of great strength."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "enchanted-moura-c02",
      "source_id": "enchanted-moura-s3",
      "quote": "Aparecem frequentemente cantando e penteando os seus longos cabelos, louros como o ouro ou negros como a noite, com um pente de ouro",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Portugis, mereka sering muncul bernyanyi sambil menyisir rambut panjangnya yang pirang seperti emas atau hitam seperti malam dengan sisir emas.",
        "en": "According to the Portuguese article, they often appear singing and combing their long hair, golden or night-black, with a golden comb."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "enchanted-moura-c03",
      "source_id": "enchanted-moura-s1",
      "quote": "According to José Leite de Vasconcelos, mouras encantadas are “beings compelled by an occult power to live on a certain state of siege as if they were numb or asleep, insofar as a particular circumstance does not break  their spell\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut José Leite de Vasconcelos, mereka makhluk yang dipaksa kekuatan gaib hidup seolah terkurung, kebas atau tertidur, selama suatu keadaan tertentu belum mematahkan kutukannya.",
        "en": "According to José Leite de Vasconcelos, they are beings forced by an occult power to live as if besieged, numb or asleep, until some circumstance breaks their spell."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "enchanted-moura-c04",
      "source_id": "enchanted-moura-s1",
      "quote": "According to ancient lore, they are the souls of young maidens who were left guarding the treasures that the males, mouros encantados (enchanted mouros) hid before heading to Mourama.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut cerita lama, mereka jiwa gadis-gadis yang ditinggalkan menjaga harta yang disembunyikan para mouros encantados sebelum pergi ke Mourama.",
        "en": "By old lore they are the souls of maidens left guarding the treasures the male mouros encantados hid before leaving for Mourama."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "enchanted-moura-c05",
      "source_id": "enchanted-moura-s1",
      "quote": "Mouras encantadas are magical maidens who guard castles, caves, bridges, wells, fountains, rivers, and treasures.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mouras encantadas adalah gadis gaib penjaga kastel, gua, jembatan, sumur, mata air, sungai, dan harta.",
        "en": "Mouras encantadas are magical maidens who guard castles, caves, bridges, wells, fountains, rivers and treasures."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "enchanted-moura-c06",
      "source_id": "enchanted-moura-s1",
      "quote": "José Leite de Vasconcelos considered as a possibility that the mouras encantadas may have had assimilated the characteristics of local deities, such as nymphs and spirits of nature. Consiglieri Pedroso also referred to the mouras encantadas as \"feminine water genies\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Leite de Vasconcelos menduga mereka menyerap ciri dewa setempat seperti nimfa dan roh alam, dan Consiglieri Pedroso menyebut mereka \"jin air perempuan\".",
        "en": "Leite de Vasconcelos thought they may have absorbed the traits of local deities such as nymphs and nature spirits, and Consiglieri Pedroso called them 'feminine water genies'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "enchanted-moura-c07",
      "source_id": "enchanted-moura-s1",
      "quote": "The fairy tales featuring mouras encantadas are thought to be of pre-Roman, Indo-European Celtic origin.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dongeng tentang mereka dianggap berasal dari tradisi Kelt Indo-Eropa pra-Romawi.",
        "en": "Tales of the mouras encantadas are thought to be of pre-Roman, Indo-European Celtic origin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "enchanted-moura-c08",
      "source_id": "enchanted-moura-s2",
      "quote": "Las mouras son criaturas fantásticas feéricas del folclore gallego y del folclore portugués de la península iberica. Su par masculino es el mouro.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, moura adalah makhluk peri dari cerita rakyat Galisia dan Portugal, dengan pasangan laki-laki bernama mouro.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, mouras are fairy beings of Galician and Portuguese folklore, whose male counterpart is the mouro."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "enchanted-moura-c09",
      "source_id": "enchanted-moura-s2",
      "quote": "La voz latina maurus (de donde el gal. mouro) para el filólogo Isodoro Millán procede del celta mrvos afín al término indoeuropeo mr-tuos, que nos conduce al latín mortuus. De ahí que muchos autores sostengan que los mouros eran razas ya desaparecidas, muertos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut filolog Isodoro Millán, kata mouro (dari bahasa Latin maurus) berasal dari bahasa Kelt mrvos yang terkait istilah Indo-Eropa mr-tuos dan bahasa Latin mortuus, sehingga banyak penulis menganggap mouros ras yang telah punah, orang mati.",
        "en": "According to the philologist Isodoro Millán, mouro (from Latin maurus) comes from Celtic mrvos, akin to Indo-European mr-tuos and Latin mortuus, so many authors hold the mouros were vanished races, the dead."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Manusia ikan Liérganes adalah pemuda Cantabria yang hilang di laut pada 1674 dan konon ditangkap di Teluk Cádiz pada 1679 dengan sisik dan insang, kisah yang diyakini benar oleh Feijoo.",
    "en": "The fish-man of Liérganes is a Cantabrian youth lost at sea in 1674 and said to have been caught in the Bay of Cádiz in 1679 with scales and gills, a story Feijoo held to be true.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "fish-man-c01",
      "fish-man-c03",
      "fish-man-c05",
      "fish-man-c06",
      "fish-man-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Menurut Feijoo, Francisco de la Vega Casar dari Liérganes, Cantabria, bekerja sebagai tukang kayu di Bilbao sampai malam Santo Yohanes 1674, ketika ia terseret arus saat berenang dan dianggap tenggelam. Pada 1679 nelayan di Teluk Cádiz menangkap makhluk berwujud pemuda berkulit putih berambut merah, bersisik di leher, perut, dan punggung serta tampak berinsang.",
      "en": "According to Feijoo, Francisco de la Vega Casar of Liérganes in Cantabria worked as a carpenter in Bilbao until St John's Eve 1674, when the current swept him away while swimming and he was thought drowned. In 1679 fishermen in the Bay of Cádiz caught a creature like a white-skinned, red-haired young man with scales on his throat, belly and back and what seemed to be gills.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "fish-man-c01",
        "fish-man-c03",
        "fish-man-c04",
        "fish-man-c05",
        "fish-man-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kisah ini pertama dicatat dalam Teatro crítico universal karya Benito Jerónimo Feijoo, yang meyakininya benar, lalu dibukukan José María Herrán pada 1877. Kini Liérganes memiliki pusat informasi dan patung manusia ikan di tepi Sungai Miera.",
      "en": "The story was first recorded in Benito Jerónimo Feijoo's Teatro crítico universal, who believed it true, and later told in a book by José María Herrán in 1877. Liérganes now has an interpretation centre and a fish-man statue on the Miera.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "fish-man-c02",
        "fish-man-c07",
        "fish-man-c08",
        "fish-man-c09"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "fish-man-c01",
      "source_id": "fish-man-s1",
      "quote": "The fish-man of Liérganes (Spanish: El hombre pez) is an entity of the mythology of Cantabria, located in the north of Spain. The fish-man would be an amphibian human-looking being, who looked a lot like a metamorphosis of a real human being who was lost at sea.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Manusia ikan Liérganes (El hombre pez) adalah sosok mitologi Cantabria, makhluk amfibi berwujud manusia yang tampak seperti hasil perubahan seorang manusia nyata yang hilang di laut.",
        "en": "The fish-man of Liérganes (El hombre pez) is a figure of Cantabrian mythology, an amphibious humanlike being that seemed the transformation of a real man lost at sea."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fish-man-c02",
      "source_id": "fish-man-s1",
      "quote": "His story was examined by Enlightenment writer Benito Jerónimo Feijoo, who claimed that the story was true.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisahnya diselidiki penulis Pencerahan Benito Jerónimo Feijoo, yang menyatakan kisah itu benar.",
        "en": "His story was examined by the Enlightenment writer Benito Jerónimo Feijoo, who claimed it was true."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fish-man-c03",
      "source_id": "fish-man-s1",
      "quote": "This son, who according to Feijoo was called Francisco de la Vega Casar, lived in Bilbao as a carpenter till 1674 when, on Saint John's Day Eve, he went with some friends to swim in Bilbao's estuary.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Feijoo, Francisco de la Vega Casar dari Liérganes bekerja sebagai tukang kayu di Bilbao sampai 1674, ketika pada malam Santo Yohanes ia berenang bersama teman-temannya di muara Bilbao.",
        "en": "According to Feijoo, Francisco de la Vega Casar of Liérganes worked as a carpenter in Bilbao until 1674, when on St John's Eve he went swimming with friends in the Bilbao estuary."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fish-man-c04",
      "source_id": "fish-man-s1",
      "quote": "He was last seen swimming away into the sea, and it was thought that he had drowned.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia terakhir terlihat berenang menjauh ke laut dan dianggap tenggelam.",
        "en": "He was last seen swimming out to sea and was thought drowned."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fish-man-c05",
      "source_id": "fish-man-s1",
      "quote": "However, five years later, in 1679, while some fishers were seafaring in the Bay of Cadiz, in southern Spain, they noticed that a strange-looking creature had become entangled with their fishing nets, and was trying to fight its way out.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lima tahun kemudian, pada 1679, nelayan di Teluk Cádiz melihat makhluk aneh tersangkut di jaring mereka dan berusaha meloloskan diri.",
        "en": "Five years later, in 1679, fishermen in the Bay of Cádiz saw a strange creature caught in their nets, struggling to escape."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fish-man-c06",
      "source_id": "fish-man-s1",
      "quote": "When they got the creature on board, they found that it had indeed a human shape: it looked like a young man, with white skin and thin red hair. However, he also showed some fish-like signs, such as a strip of scales that went down from his throat to his stomach, another one that covered his spine, and what seemingly were gills around his neck.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah ditangkap dengan umpan roti, makhluk itu ternyata berwujud pemuda berkulit putih berambut merah tipis, tetapi bersisik dari leher sampai perut dan di sepanjang tulang punggung, serta tampak berinsang di lehernya.",
        "en": "Once caught with bread, the creature proved to be a young man with white skin and thin red hair, but with a strip of scales from throat to belly and down his spine, and what looked like gills on his neck."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fish-man-c07",
      "source_id": "fish-man-s2",
      "quote": "La primera reseña en la que aparece el relato del hombre pez es en el volumen VI del Teatro crítico universal de Fray Benito Jerónimo Feijoo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, catatan pertama kisah manusia ikan ada dalam jilid VI Teatro crítico universal karya Fray Benito Jerónimo Feijoo.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, the first account of the fish-man is in volume VI of Fray Benito Jerónimo Feijoo's Teatro crítico universal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fish-man-c08",
      "source_id": "fish-man-s2",
      "quote": "Posteriormente José María Herrán escribió un libro titulado El hombre-pez de Liérganes (Santander, 1877), basado en esta historia tradicional popular. Actualmente existe un centro de interpretación en Liérganes, emplazado en un antiguo molino",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "José María Herrán kemudian menulis buku El hombre-pez de Liérganes (Santander, 1877), dan kini ada pusat informasi tentangnya di bekas penggilingan di Liérganes.",
        "en": "José María Herrán later wrote the book El hombre-pez de Liérganes (Santander, 1877), and there is now an interpretation centre in an old mill at Liérganes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fish-man-c09",
      "source_id": "fish-man-s3",
      "quote": "Позднее, статуя человека-рыбы, установленная на берегу Мьеры, стала одной из основных достопримечательностей Льерганеса.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Rusia menyebut patung manusia ikan di tepi Sungai Miera menjadi salah satu daya tarik utama Liérganes.",
        "en": "The Russian article says the fish-man statue on the bank of the Miera became one of Liérganes' main sights."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Gjenganger adalah mayat hidup dalam cerita rakyat Skandinavia, orang mati berwujud jasmani yang kembali dari kubur karena terbunuh, bunuh diri, atau urusan yang belum selesai.",
    "en": "The gjenganger is the Scandinavian revenant, a bodily dead person who returns from the grave because of murder, suicide or unfinished business.",

      "gjenganger-c04",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Gjenganger, \"yang berjalan lagi\", adalah sebutan Nordik untuk mayat hidup, dengan bentuk genganger di Denmark, gengångare di Swedia, dan afturganga di Islandia. Berbeda dari hantu, ia berwujud sepenuhnya jasmani. Korban pembunuhan, pembunuhnya, orang yang bunuh diri, dan mereka yang meninggalkan urusan belum selesai kembali dari kubur dan sering butuh bantuan orang hidup untuk tenang.",
      "en": "The gjenganger, 'again-walker', is the Nordic term for a revenant, as genganger in Danish, gengångare in Swedish and afturganga in Icelandic. Unlike a ghost, it has an entirely bodily form. Murder victims, their killers, suicides and those with unfinished business come back from the grave and often need a living person's help to find peace.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "gjenganger-c01",
      "source_id": "gjenganger-s1",
      "quote": "In Nordic folklore; Danish: genganger, Norwegian: gjenganger, Swedish: gengångare (\"(a)gain-walker\"), among more, is a term for a revenant or ghost, meaning \"someone which goes again\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Nordik, genganger (Denmark), gjenganger (Norwegia), atau gengångare (Swedia), \"yang berjalan lagi\", adalah sebutan untuk mayat hidup atau hantu.",
        "en": "In Nordic folklore, genganger (Danish), gjenganger (Norwegian) or gengångare (Swedish), 'again-walker', is a term for a revenant or ghost."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gjenganger-c02",
      "source_id": "gjenganger-s1",
      "quote": "Other forms includes Icelandic: afturganga, Norwegian: attergangar, stemming from Old Norse: aptrgangr (\"re-walker\"); Danish: genfærd, Norwegian: gjenferd (\"again-travel or after-journey\").",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bentuk lain adalah afturganga (Islandia) dan attergangar (Norwegia) dari bahasa Norse Kuno aptrgangr, serta genfærd (Denmark) dan gjenferd (Norwegia).",
        "en": "Other forms are Icelandic afturganga and Norwegian attergangar from Old Norse aptrgangr, and Danish genfærd and Norwegian gjenferd."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gjenganger-c03",
      "source_id": "gjenganger-s1",
      "quote": "A gjenganger could have several reasons to return from the afterlife. Murdered people and their murderers could seldom sleep peacefully in their graves. People who had committed suicide often came back as gjengangere.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gjenganger kembali dari alam baka karena berbagai sebab: korban dan pembunuhnya jarang tenang di kubur, dan orang yang bunuh diri sering kembali sebagai gjengangere.",
        "en": "A gjenganger might return from the afterlife for several reasons: the murdered and their murderers seldom rested in their graves, and suicides often came back as gjengangere."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gjenganger-c04",
      "source_id": "gjenganger-s1",
      "quote": "At other times, people came back from the grave because they had left something undone. Most often they needed someone to help them do this, before they could finally be at peace.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada pula yang kembali karena meninggalkan urusan yang belum selesai, dan biasanya mereka membutuhkan bantuan seseorang sebelum akhirnya tenang.",
        "en": "Others came back because they had left something undone, and usually needed someone's help before they could rest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gjenganger-c05",
      "source_id": "gjenganger-s1",
      "quote": "The gjenganger in the Scandinavian tradition took on an entirely corporeal form. It normally had no spectre-like qualities whatsoever.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam tradisi Skandinavia, gjenganger berwujud sepenuhnya jasmani dan biasanya sama sekali tidak seperti hantu.",
        "en": "In Scandinavian tradition the gjenganger had an entirely bodily form and usually no ghostly qualities at all."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gjenganger-c06",
      "source_id": "gjenganger-s2",
      "quote": "Cette créature semble originaire de la mythologie nordique, puisque de telles créatures sont déjà citées dans certaines sagas islandaises médiévales, à l'exemple de la saga Grettis, la saga Eyrbyggja ou la saga d'Erik le Rouge.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, makhluk ini tampaknya berasal dari mitologi Nordik karena sudah disebut dalam saga Islandia abad pertengahan seperti saga Grettis, saga Eyrbyggja, dan saga Erik si Merah.",
        "en": "According to the French article, the creature seems to come from Norse mythology, since such beings already appear in medieval Icelandic sagas such as Grettis saga, Eyrbyggja saga and the saga of Erik the Red."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "gjenganger-c07",
      "source_id": "gjenganger-s3",
      "quote": "Un Gjenganger (in Norvegese: Gjenganger, Attergangar o Gjenferd; in lingua danese: Genganger o Genfærd; in svedese: Gengångare) è un revenant del folclore scandinavo, un fantasma tornato dalla tomba.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Italia menyebut gjenganger revenant dalam cerita rakyat Skandinavia, hantu yang kembali dari kubur.",
        "en": "The Italian article calls the gjenganger a revenant of Scandinavian folklore, a ghost returned from the grave."
      }
    }
```

```json
    "en": "The hercinia is a legendary bird of the Hercynian Forest whose feathers shine like fire at night, recorded by Pliny the Elder and the medieval bestiaries.",

Plinius Tua pada abad ke-1 menulis bahwa bulunya bersinar seperti api pada malam hari, dan pada abad ke-7 Isidorus dari Sevilla menyebut bulu yang diletakkan di tanah memancarkan cahaya penunjuk jalan. In the 1st century Pliny the Elder wrote that its feathers shine like fire at night, and in the 7th century Isidore of Seville said feathers laid on the ground give light to show the way.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "hercinia-c01",
      "source_id": "hercinia-s1",
      "quote": "Hercinia is a legendary bird with glowing feathers that inhabited the Hercynian Forest of ancient Germany.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hercinia adalah burung legendaris berbulu bercahaya yang menghuni Hutan Hercynia di Germania kuno.",
        "en": "The hercinia is a legendary bird with glowing feathers that lived in the Hercynian Forest of ancient Germany."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hercinia-c02",
      "source_id": "hercinia-s1",
      "quote": "The hercinia is depicted in Medieval bestiaries and was recorded in the 1st century by Pliny the Elder, who wrote, \"In the Hercynian Forest, in Germany, we hear of a singular kind of bird, the feathers of which shine at night like fire\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hercinia digambarkan dalam bestiari Abad Pertengahan dan dicatat pada abad ke-1 oleh Plinius Tua: \"Di Hutan Hercynia, di Germania, terdengar kabar tentang sejenis burung aneh yang bulunya bersinar seperti api pada malam hari.\"",
        "en": "The hercinia appears in Medieval bestiaries and was recorded in the 1st century by Pliny the Elder: 'In the Hercynian Forest, in Germany, we hear of a singular kind of bird, the feathers of which shine at night like fire.'"
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hercinia-c03",
      "source_id": "hercinia-s1",
      "quote": "Writing in the 7th century, Isidore of Seville notes, \"Their feathers sparkle so much in the shade that, however dark the night is with thick shadows, these feathers, when placed on the ground, give off light that helps to mark the way, and the sign of the glittering feathers makes clear the direction of the path\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada abad ke-7 Isidorus dari Sevilla menulis bahwa bulunya begitu berkilau sehingga, segelap apa pun malam, bulu yang diletakkan di tanah memancarkan cahaya penunjuk jalan.",
        "en": "Writing in the 7th century, Isidore of Seville notes that its feathers sparkle so that, however dark the night, feathers laid on the ground give off light that marks the path."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hercinia-c04",
      "source_id": "hercinia-s1",
      "quote": "It is possible that the reports of the hercinia were based on sightings of birds with iridescent feathers that reflected moonlight, a phenomenon that has been reported of a number of species of birds, including the barn owl and the bittern.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporan tentang hercinia mungkin berasal dari burung berbulu warna-warni yang memantulkan cahaya bulan, gejala yang dilaporkan pada beberapa spesies seperti burung hantu serak dan burung bittern.",
        "en": "Reports of the hercinia may come from birds with iridescent feathers reflecting moonlight, as reported of several species including the barn owl and the bittern."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hercinia-c05",
      "source_id": "hercinia-s2",
      "quote": "La légende de ces oiseaux lumineux présents dans la forêt Hercynienne a inspiré de nombreuses œuvres d’art, notamment le célèbre poème de Thomas Moore : A Dream of Antiquity.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, legenda burung bercahaya ini mengilhami banyak karya seni, termasuk puisi Thomas Moore A Dream of Antiquity.",
        "en": "According to the French article, the legend inspired many works of art, notably Thomas Moore's poem A Dream of Antiquity."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hercinia-c06",
      "source_id": "hercinia-s3",
      "quote": "Ellos se encontraban en el bosque Hercínico, en el sur de Alemania, donde sus plumas brillantes mantenían sendas encendidas en la espesura del bosque.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, burung-burung ini tinggal di Hutan Hercynia di Jerman selatan, tempat bulu bercahayanya menerangi jalan setapak di hutan lebat.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, they lived in the Hercynian Forest in southern Germany, where their glowing feathers lit the paths through the thick forest."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Inguma adalah dewa mimpi yang jahat dalam mitologi Basque, yang masuk ke rumah pada malam hari, duduk di dada orang tidur, dan mencekik mereka.",
    "en": "Inguma is the malevolent Basque god of dreams who enters houses at night, sits on sleepers' chests and chokes them.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "inguma-c01",
      "inguma-c04",
      "inguma-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Inguma, atau Mauma di Baigorri, adalah dewa mimpi dan irelu jahat dalam mitologi Basque. Ia masuk ke rumah pada malam hari saat orang tidur, duduk di dada mereka, dan mencekik leher hingga sulit bernapas, menimbulkan mimpi buruk dan kadang kematian. Asal-usulnya mungkin kelumpuhan tidur.",
      "en": "Inguma, or Mauma in Baigorri, is the Basque god of dreams and an evil irelu. He enters houses at night while people sleep, sits on their chests and squeezes their throats so they can hardly breathe, causing nightmares and sometimes death. He may have arisen from sleep paralysis.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "inguma-c01",
        "inguma-c03",
        "inguma-c04",
        "inguma-c05",
        "inguma-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Orang menangkalnya dengan doa kepada Santo Andreas dan Santa Agnes, dan ia kadang disamakan dengan iblis Aideko. Ia disebut dalam film Ofrenda a la tormenta.",
      "en": "People warded him off with prayers to St Andrew and St Agnes, and he was sometimes equated with the demon Aideko. He is mentioned in the film Ofrenda a la tormenta.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "inguma-c02",
        "inguma-c07",
        "inguma-c08"
      ]
    }
```
