  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "inguma-c01",
      "source_id": "inguma-s1",
      "quote": "Inguma (Mauma in Baigorri) is the god of dreams in Basque mythology and religion.  He is regarded as a malevolent force who enters houses at night and plagues residents with nightmares, sometimes killing them.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inguma (Mauma di Baigorri) adalah dewa mimpi dalam mitologi dan agama Basque, kekuatan jahat yang masuk ke rumah pada malam hari dan menyiksa penghuni dengan mimpi buruk, kadang sampai membunuh mereka.",
        "en": "Inguma (Mauma in Baigorri) is the god of dreams in Basque mythology and religion, a malevolent force that enters houses at night and torments the residents with nightmares, sometimes killing them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inguma-c02",
      "source_id": "inguma-s1",
      "quote": "He is mentioned in Ofrenda a la tormenta (Offering to the Storm), the third film in the Baztán trilogy by Dolores Redondo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia disebut dalam Ofrenda a la tormenta, film ketiga trilogi Baztán karya Dolores Redondo.",
        "en": "He is mentioned in Ofrenda a la tormenta, the third film of Dolores Redondo's Baztán trilogy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inguma-c03",
      "source_id": "inguma-s2",
      "quote": "Inguma, ou Mauma à Baigorri, est un des Irelus ou génies maléfiques qui composent le vaste panthéon de la mythologie basque.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, Inguma adalah salah satu irelu atau roh jahat dalam jajaran makhluk mitologi Basque.",
        "en": "According to the French article, Inguma is one of the irelus or evil spirits of the wide Basque pantheon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inguma-c04",
      "source_id": "inguma-s2",
      "quote": "Sa nature est mauvaise, puisque son seul but est de noyer ses victimes pendant leur sommeil. Il apparaît de nuit dans les maisons, lorsque les gens sont endormis. Il serre la gorge de certains d'entre eux, leur rendant la respiration difficile, provoquant ainsi une indicible angoisse.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sifatnya jahat karena tujuannya hanya mencekik korbannya saat tidur: ia muncul di rumah pada malam hari dan mencekik leher orang yang tidur sehingga sulit bernapas dan sangat ketakutan.",
        "en": "His nature is evil, his only aim to choke his victims in their sleep: he appears in houses at night and squeezes sleepers' throats, making breathing hard and causing unspeakable dread."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inguma-c05",
      "source_id": "inguma-s2",
      "quote": "L'origine anthropologique de ce génie pourrait être recherchée dans le phénomène appelé « paralysie du sommeil »",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Asal-usul antropologis roh ini mungkin terletak pada kelumpuhan tidur.",
        "en": "The anthropological origin of this spirit may lie in sleep paralysis."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inguma-c06",
      "source_id": "inguma-s2",
      "quote": "ce qui coïncide avec le schéma suivi par Inguma, qui s'assoit sur la poitrine de ses victimes et leur serre la gorge avec ses mains.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inguma duduk di dada korbannya dan mencekik leher mereka dengan tangannya.",
        "en": "Inguma sits on his victims' chests and squeezes their throats with his hands."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inguma-c07",
      "source_id": "inguma-s3",
      "quote": "Ochroną przed demonem była modllitwa do św. Andrzeja i św. Agnieszki.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Polandia, perlindungan terhadap iblis ini adalah doa kepada Santo Andreas dan Santa Agnes.",
        "en": "According to the Polish article, the protection against this demon was a prayer to St Andrew and St Agnes."
      }
    },

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Martin Txiki adalah penipu cerdik sekaligus pahlawan pembawa peradaban dalam mitologi Basque yang mencuri benih gandum dan rahasia gergaji dari Basajaunak.",
    "en": "Martin Txiki is the trickster and civilising hero of Basque mythology who stole wheat seed and the secret of the saw from the Basajaunak.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "San Martin Txiki, \"Santo Martin Kecil\", adalah penipu cerdik dan pahlawan pembawa peradaban Basque, yang namanya berasal dari Santo Martinus dari Tours; di Aragon ia disebut San Martinico. Ia mencuri rahasia bertani dari Basajaunak, penguasa hutan: ia bertaruh dapat melompati tumpukan gandum, sengaja jatuh dengan sepatu besar, dan membawa pulang benih yang terselip. Dalam versi Ataun, kapak yang dilempar Basajaun meleset dan menancap di pohon kastanye.",
      "en": "San Martin Txiki, 'Little Saint Martin', is the Basque trickster and civilising hero, his name taken from St Martin of Tours; in Aragon he is San Martinico. He stole farming secrets from the Basajaunak, lords of the woods: betting he could jump a heap of wheat, he fell in on purpose in big shoes and carried home the seeds caught in them. In the Ataun version the Basajaun's thrown axe missed and stuck in a chestnut tree.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "martintxiki-c01",
      "source_id": "martintxiki-s1",
      "quote": "San Martin Txiki (\"Little Saint Martin\") is the trickster figure from Basque mythology. Txiki (pronounced \"cheeky\" [ˈt͡ʃiːki]) means \"little\" in an affectionate sense.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "San Martin Txiki (\"Santo Martin Kecil\") adalah tokoh penipu cerdik dalam mitologi Basque; txiki berarti \"kecil\" dalam arti sayang.",
        "en": "San Martin Txiki ('Little Saint Martin') is the trickster of Basque mythology; txiki means 'little' in an affectionate sense."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "martintxiki-c02",
      "source_id": "martintxiki-s1",
      "quote": "He stole the secrets of planting, sowing, and harvesting from the Basajaunak (lords of the woods). He also invented the first saws, modeling them after the edges of the chestnut leaf.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia mencuri rahasia menanam, menabur, dan memanen dari Basajaunak (penguasa hutan), dan menciptakan gergaji pertama meniru tepi daun kastanye.",
        "en": "He stole the secrets of planting, sowing and harvesting from the Basajaunak (lords of the woods), and invented the first saws on the model of the chestnut leaf's edge."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "martintxiki-c03",
      "source_id": "martintxiki-s1",
      "quote": "San Martin Txiki also exists in Aragonese mythology in the valleys of Tena, Ansó and Broto (places where local toponymy derives from Basque) under the name San Martinico.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "San Martin Txiki juga ada dalam mitologi Aragon di lembah Tena, Ansó, dan Broto dengan nama San Martinico.",
        "en": "San Martin Txiki also exists in Aragonese mythology in the Tena, Ansó and Broto valleys as San Martinico."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "martintxiki-c04",
      "source_id": "martintxiki-s1",
      "quote": "It is said that the Basajaunak kept all the wheat for themselves, so San Martin Txiki bet them that he could he could jump over a pile of wheat seeds without touching it.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Basajaunak menyimpan semua gandum untuk diri mereka, lalu San Martin Txiki bertaruh bahwa ia bisa melompati tumpukan benih gandum tanpa menyentuhnya.",
        "en": "The Basajaunak kept all the wheat for themselves, so San Martin Txiki bet them he could jump a pile of wheat seed without touching it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "martintxiki-c05",
      "source_id": "martintxiki-s1",
      "quote": "However, he had worn large shoes that day, and collected some wheat seeds in them when he fell in. The Basajaunak were so busy laughing at him that they didn't notice, and he brought the seeds back to the humans.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia sengaja jatuh ke tumpukan itu dengan memakai sepatu besar sehingga benih gandum masuk ke sepatunya; Basajaunak sibuk menertawakannya dan tidak sadar, lalu ia membawa benih itu kepada manusia.",
        "en": "He fell in on purpose wearing big shoes, so wheat seeds collected in them; the Basajaunak, busy laughing, did not notice, and he brought the seeds to humans."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "martintxiki-c06",
      "source_id": "martintxiki-s1",
      "quote": "The Basajaunak asked his servant if he had been inspired by the shape of chestnut leaves. The servant told him what they had said, and San Martin Txiki was then able to make one himself.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rahasia gergaji ia dapat lewat pelayannya: Basajaunak bertanya apakah ia terilhami daun kastanye, dan dari situ San Martin Txiki membuat gergajinya sendiri.",
        "en": "He got the saw through his servant: the Basajaunak asked whether he had been inspired by chestnut leaves, and from that San Martin Txiki made one himself."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "martintxiki-c07",
      "source_id": "martintxiki-s2",
      "quote": "Martin txiki (Martin le petit), Martintxo, San Martin txiki (saint Martin le petit) ou San Martiniko est considéré comme le héros « civilisateur » du peuple basque. Ce nom est une transformation de celui de saint Martin, un saint tourangeau qui figure dans un thème légendaire.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, Martin Txiki, Martintxo, atau San Martiniko dianggap pahlawan pembawa peradaban bagi bangsa Basque, dan namanya berasal dari Santo Martinus dari Tours.",
        "en": "According to the French article, Martin Txiki, Martintxo or San Martiniko is seen as the civilising hero of the Basque people, his name a transformation of St Martin of Tours."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "martintxiki-c08",
      "source_id": "martintxiki-s2",
      "quote": "Il lança dans sa direction son arme de jet, une hache. Ratant Martín Txiki, celle-ci se planta dans le tronc d'un châtaignier, au lieu-dit Mekolalde à San Gregorio d'Ataun",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam versi Ataun, seorang Basajaun yang menyadari pencurian itu melemparkan kapak, tetapi meleset dan menancap di batang kastanye di Mekolalde, San Gregorio d'Ataun.",
        "en": "In the Ataun version a Basajaun who noticed the theft threw an axe, which missed and stuck in a chestnut trunk at Mekolalde, San Gregorio d'Ataun."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "martintxiki-c09",
      "source_id": "martintxiki-s3",
      "quote": "Este trickster probablemente lo creara la imaginación popular como arquetipo de los sanadores celtibéricos de la zona del Moncayo (más en concreto de los de San Martín de la Virgen de Moncayo), que viajaban a tierras vasconas y/o pirenaicas a curar a los enfermos con herramientas de bronce, cobre y hierro",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, tokoh penipu ini mungkin diciptakan imajinasi rakyat sebagai arketipe tabib Keltiberia dari daerah Moncayo yang berkelana ke negeri Basque dan Pyrenees dengan perkakas perunggu, tembaga, dan besi.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, this trickster was probably created by popular imagination as an archetype of the Celtiberian healers of the Moncayo area who travelled to Basque and Pyrenean lands with bronze, copper and iron tools."
      }
    }
  ],

      "source_id": "michigan-dogman-s1",
      "quote": "In folklore, the Michigan Dogman is an alleged creature first witnessed in 1887 in Wexford County, Michigan, United States. It was described as a seven-foot-tall (2.1 m), blue- or amber-eyed bipedal canid possessing the torso of a man with a fearsome howl resembling a human scream.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat, Michigan Dogman adalah makhluk yang konon pertama terlihat pada 1887 di Wexford County, Michigan, Amerika Serikat, digambarkan sebagai anjing berkaki dua setinggi tujuh kaki (2,1 m) bermata biru atau kuning, bertubuh manusia, dengan lolongan seperti jeritan manusia.",
        "en": "In folklore the Michigan Dogman is an alleged creature first seen in 1887 in Wexford County, Michigan, United States, described as a seven-foot (2.1 m), blue- or amber-eyed two-legged canine with a man's torso and a howl like a human scream."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "michigan-dogman-c02",
      "source_id": "michigan-dogman-s1",
      "quote": "Folklore claims that the creature reemerges on a recurring ten-year pattern linked to years ending in the number seven.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut cerita rakyat, makhluk ini muncul kembali setiap sepuluh tahun pada tahun yang berakhiran angka tujuh.",
        "en": "Folklore says the creature reappears every ten years, in years ending in seven."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "michigan-dogman-c03",
      "source_id": "michigan-dogman-s1",
      "quote": "In 1987, disc jockey Steve Cook at WTCM-FM in Traverse City, Michigan, recorded a song titled \"The Legend\", which he initially played as an April Fool's Day joke.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 1987 penyiar radio Steve Cook di WTCM-FM, Traverse City, merekam lagu The Legend yang mula-mula diputar sebagai lelucon April Mop.",
        "en": "In 1987 disc jockey Steve Cook of WTCM-FM in Traverse City recorded a song, The Legend, first played as an April Fool's joke."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "michigan-dogman-c04",
      "source_id": "michigan-dogman-s1",
      "quote": "I made it up completely from my own imagination as an April Fools' prank for the radio and stumbled my way to a legend that goes back all the way to Native American times.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cook mengaku mengarang lagu itu sepenuhnya dari imajinasinya sebagai lelucon April Mop untuk radio.",
        "en": "Cook said he made it up entirely from his own imagination as an April Fools' prank for the radio."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "michigan-dogman-c05",
      "source_id": "michigan-dogman-s1",
      "quote": "After he played the song, Cook received calls from listeners who said that they had encountered a similar creature.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah lagu itu diputar, Cook menerima telepon dari pendengar yang mengaku pernah bertemu makhluk serupa.",
        "en": "After he played the song, Cook got calls from listeners who said they had met a similar creature."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "michigan-dogman-c06",
      "source_id": "michigan-dogman-s2",
      "quote": "L'Homme chien du Michigan (Dogman of Michigan) animal bipède ressemblant à un canidé issu du folklore du Michigan et née d'une chanson locale sortie en 1987 faisant potentiellement écho à des récits plus anciens.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, Dogman adalah hewan berkaki dua mirip anjing dari cerita rakyat Michigan yang lahir dari lagu lokal tahun 1987, mungkin menggemakan cerita yang lebih tua.",
        "en": "According to the French article, the Dogman is a two-legged dog-like animal of Michigan folklore born of a 1987 local song, possibly echoing older tales."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "michigan-dogman-c07",
      "source_id": "michigan-dogman-s2",
      "quote": "Selon Cook, une légende amérindienne existerait, ce qui fait dire que l'homme chien rode autour de la rivière Manistee depuis l'époque où les tribus Odawa y vivaient bien qu'aucune source authentique n'ait jamais été documentée.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Cook ada legenda penduduk asli bahwa makhluk ini berkeliaran di sekitar Sungai Manistee sejak zaman suku Odawa, tetapi tidak ada sumber autentik yang pernah terdokumentasi.",
        "en": "According to Cook a Native legend has the dogman roaming the Manistee River since Odawa times, though no authentic source has ever been documented."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "michigan-dogman-c08",
      "source_id": "michigan-dogman-s3",
      "quote": "В криптозоологической литературе догмена относят к предполагаемым прямоходящим псовоподобным существам, тогда как фольклористы рассматривают его прежде всего как персонажа современной региональной легенды.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Rusia, literatur kriptozoologi menggolongkannya makhluk mirip anjing yang berjalan tegak, sedangkan para folkloris memandangnya terutama sebagai tokoh legenda regional modern.",
        "en": "According to the Russian article, cryptozoological literature classes the dogman as an alleged upright canid, while folklorists see it mainly as a figure of modern regional legend."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Muladona adalah bagal betina berkepala perempuan dari mitologi Katalan, gadis terkutuk yang menyusup ke rombongan bagal pada malam hari dan membuat mereka berlari ke jurang.",
    "en": "The Muladona is a woman-headed female mule of Catalan mythology, a cursed girl who slips into mule trains at night and drives them over ravines.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "muladona-c01",
      "muladona-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Muladona atau Donamula, \"bagal-perempuan\", adalah gadis yang dikutuk warga desanya menjadi bagal karena tidak hormat dan tidak beragama. Ia tampak seperti bagal tetapi bersurai seperti rambut perempuan, berwajah manusia, dan berpayudara. Pada malam hari ia bergabung dengan rombongan bagal di pegunungan sehingga bagal-bagal ketakutan dan jatuh ke jurang.",
      "en": "The Muladona or Donamula, 'mule-woman', is a girl her townsfolk cursed into a mule for being irreverent and irreligious. She looks like a mule but has a woman's hair for a mane, a human face and breasts. At night she joins mule trains in the mountains, so the mules panic and fall into ravines.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "muladona-c01",
        "muladona-c02",
        "muladona-c03",
        "muladona-c04",
        "muladona-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kusir bagal memberi hewannya roti berbentuk Muladona agar tidak takut, dan terus menghitung bagal mereka agar ia tidak menyusup tanpa ketahuan.",
      "en": "Muleteers fed their animals Muladona-shaped bread so they would not take fright, and kept counting their mules so she could not slip in unseen.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "muladona-c05",
        "muladona-c06"

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "muladona-c01",
      "source_id": "muladona-s1",
      "quote": "In Catalan mythology, a Muladona or Donamula (mule-woman or woman-mule in Catalan) is a female mule with a human woman's head. Legend has it that a young woman was cursed by the townspeople for being irreverent and irreligious, and the curse turned her into a mule.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, Muladona atau Donamula (\"bagal-perempuan\") adalah bagal betina berkepala perempuan; menurut legenda, seorang gadis yang tidak hormat dan tidak beragama dikutuk warga desanya menjadi bagal.",
        "en": "In Catalan mythology the Muladona or Donamula ('mule-woman') is a female mule with a woman's head; by legend a young woman cursed by her townsfolk for being irreverent and irreligious was turned into a mule."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muladona-c02",
      "source_id": "muladona-s1",
      "quote": "According to legend, she wandered mountains, especially at night, joining mule trains. It was claimed that mules were frightened by her presence enough to stampede into ravines and die.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia berkeliaran di pegunungan, terutama malam hari, dan bergabung dengan rombongan bagal, yang ketakutan hingga berlari ke jurang dan mati.",
        "en": "She roamed the mountains, especially at night, joining mule trains, whose mules were so frightened they stampeded into ravines and died."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muladona-c03",
      "source_id": "muladona-s1",
      "quote": "The Muladona looks like a mule, but it is a mixture of human and animal, its mane is like a woman's hair and its face, while still appearing to be like that of an animal, is obviously human.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Muladona tampak seperti bagal tetapi campuran manusia dan hewan: surainya seperti rambut perempuan dan wajahnya, walau seperti hewan, jelas manusia.",
        "en": "The Muladona looks like a mule but is a mix of human and animal: her mane is like a woman's hair, and her face, though animal-like, is clearly human."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muladona-c04",
      "source_id": "muladona-s1",
      "quote": "It is always represented with the four legs of a mule, but sometimes the front two legs join a thorax, located where a mule would have its neck. In addition, the animal is always represented with breasts to underline that it is female.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia selalu digambarkan berkaki empat bagal, kadang dengan kaki depan menyatu pada dada manusia di tempat leher bagal, dan selalu berpayudara untuk menegaskan bahwa ia perempuan.",
        "en": "She is always shown with a mule's four legs, sometimes with the front legs joined to a chest where a mule's neck would be, and always with breasts to stress she is female."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muladona-c05",
      "source_id": "muladona-s1",
      "quote": "The muleteers used to give their mules small Muladona-shaped pieces of bread to eat, in the belief that this would stop the animals being frightened and allow them to ignore her if she appeared.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para kusir bagal memberi bagal mereka roti kecil berbentuk Muladona agar hewan itu tidak takut dan mengabaikannya bila ia muncul.",
        "en": "Muleteers fed their mules little Muladona-shaped breads so the animals would not take fright and would ignore her if she appeared."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muladona-c06",
      "source_id": "muladona-s2",
      "quote": "Así y todo, durante la marcha contaban las cabezas que llevaban para evitar que la muladona se hubiese mezclado con sus animales sin que ellos se diesen cuenta y acabasen pagándolo caro.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, selama perjalanan mereka terus menghitung bagal agar Muladona tidak menyusup tanpa ketahuan.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, during the journey they kept counting their animals so the Muladona would not slip in among them unnoticed."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muladona-c07",
      "source_id": "muladona-s3",
      "quote": "কাটালন পৌৰাণিক কাহিনীত মুলাডোনা বা ডোনামুলা বুলিও কোৱা (কাটালান ভাষাত খচ্চৰ-মহিলা বা মহিলা-খচ্চৰ) হৈছে মানৱ নাৰীৰ মূৰ থকা এটা মাইকী খচ্চৰ।",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Assam juga menyebut Muladona bagal betina berkepala perempuan dalam mitologi Katalan.",
        "en": "The Assamese article also calls the Muladona a female mule with a woman's head in Catalan mythology."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Revenant adalah roh atau mayat hidup dalam cerita rakyat Eropa yang bangkit dari kubur untuk menghantui orang hidup, kepercayaan yang jejaknya terlihat dalam kuburan sejak Zaman Perunggu.",
    "en": "A revenant is a spirit or animated corpse of European folklore that rises from the grave to haunt the living, a belief traced in graves back to the Bronze Age.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "revenant-c01",
      "revenant-c03",
      "revenant-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Revenant, \"yang kembali\", adalah roh atau mayat hidup yang dipercaya bangkit untuk menghantui orang hidup, dikenal dalam berbagai budaya termasuk Kelt dan Norse dan dicatat sejarawan Inggris abad pertengahan. Kematian tidak wajar dianggap penyebab arwah mengembara. Banyak folkloris menganggapnya istilah umum untuk mayat hidup, meski sebagian membedakannya dari vampir Eropa Timur.",
      "en": "The revenant, 'one who returns', is a spirit or animated corpse believed to rise to haunt the living, known in many cultures including Celtic and Norse and recorded by medieval English historians. An unusual death was thought to make the dead wander. Many folklorists treat it as a general term for the undead, though some distinguish it from the Eastern European vampire.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "revenant-c01",
        "revenant-c02",
        "revenant-c05",
        "revenant-c07",
        "revenant-c08",
        "revenant-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Arkeolog menemukan kuburan revenant di seluruh Eropa, dengan batu di atas kaki atau di rahang dan bagian tubuh yang dibuang, yang tertua dari Zaman Perunggu sekitar 4.000 tahun lalu. Kepercayaan ini tercatat dalam sastra Romawi, umum di Abad Pertengahan, kuat di Polandia abad ke-17, dan dikaji Augustin Calmet pada 1751.",
      "en": "Archaeologists have found revenant graves throughout Europe, with stones on the legs or in the jaw and body parts removed, the oldest from the Bronze Age about 4,000 years ago. The belief appears in Roman literature, was common in the Middle Ages, strong in 17th-century Poland, and studied by Augustin Calmet in 1751.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "revenant-c03",
        "revenant-c04",
        "revenant-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "revenant-c01",
      "source_id": "revenant-s1",
      "quote": "In folklore, a revenant is a spirit or animated corpse that is believed to have been resurrected to haunt the living. The word revenant is derived from the Old French word revenant 'returning' (see also the related French verb revenir 'to come back').",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat, revenant adalah roh atau mayat hidup yang dipercaya bangkit untuk menghantui orang hidup; kata revenant berasal dari bahasa Prancis Kuno, \"yang kembali\".",
        "en": "In folklore a revenant is a spirit or animated corpse believed to have risen to haunt the living; the word comes from Old French revenant, 'returning'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "revenant-c02",
      "source_id": "revenant-s1",
      "quote": "Revenants are part of the legend of various cultures, including Celtic and Norse mythology, and stories of supposed revenant visitations were documented by English historians in the Middle Ages.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Revenant ada dalam legenda berbagai budaya, termasuk mitologi Kelt dan Norse, dan kisah kunjungan revenant dicatat sejarawan Inggris pada Abad Pertengahan.",
        "en": "Revenants feature in the legends of various cultures, including Celtic and Norse mythology, and tales of revenant visits were recorded by English historians in the Middle Ages."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "revenant-c03",
      "source_id": "revenant-s1",
      "quote": "Archaeologists have found revenant graves throughout Europe, characterized by bodies that had precautions taken to prevent them from rising up and causing mischief for the living, such as stones placed over the legs, stones placed in the jaw so it could not speak, bodies lodged with bricks, or body parts removed.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Arkeolog menemukan kuburan revenant di seluruh Eropa, berisi jasad yang diberi pencegahan agar tidak bangkit, seperti batu di atas kaki, batu di rahang agar tak bisa bicara, jasad yang diganjal bata, atau bagian tubuh yang dibuang.",
        "en": "Archaeologists have found revenant graves across Europe, with bodies given precautions against rising, such as stones on the legs, stones in the jaw so it could not speak, bodies wedged with bricks or body parts removed."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "revenant-c04",
      "source_id": "revenant-s1",
      "quote": "The oldest known graves are as old as 4,000 years BP from the Bronze Age. Roman literature contained writings about revenants. They were common throughout the Middle Ages, and 17th-century Poland was reportedly a hot-bed of revenant superstition.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuburan tertua berasal dari sekitar 4.000 tahun lalu pada Zaman Perunggu; sastra Romawi menulis tentang revenant, kepercayaan ini umum sepanjang Abad Pertengahan, dan Polandia abad ke-17 konon menjadi pusatnya.",
        "en": "The oldest known graves date to about 4,000 years BP in the Bronze Age; Roman literature wrote of revenants, they were common through the Middle Ages, and 17th-century Poland was reportedly a hotbed of the belief."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "revenant-c05",
      "source_id": "revenant-s1",
      "quote": "The term \"revenant\" has been used interchangeably with \"ghost\" by folklorists. While some maintain that vampires derive from Eastern European folklore and revenants derive from Western European folklore, many assert that revenant is a generic term for the undead.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Folkloris memakai istilah revenant bergantian dengan hantu; sebagian berpendapat vampir berasal dari Eropa Timur dan revenant dari Eropa Barat, tetapi banyak yang menganggap revenant istilah umum untuk mayat hidup.",
        "en": "Folklorists have used 'revenant' interchangeably with 'ghost'; some say vampires come from Eastern and revenants from Western European folklore, but many hold revenant is a general term for the undead."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "revenant-c06",
      "source_id": "revenant-s1",
      "quote": "Augustin Calmet conducted extensive research on the topic in his work titled Traité sur les apparitions des esprits et sur les vampires ou les revenans de Hongrie, de Moravie, &c. (1751) in which he describes the related rumors of the time.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Augustin Calmet meneliti topik ini secara luas dalam Traité sur les apparitions des esprits et sur les vampires ou les revenans de Hongrie, de Moravie (1751).",
        "en": "Augustin Calmet researched the subject at length in his Traité sur les apparitions des esprits et sur les vampires ou les revenans de Hongrie, de Moravie (1751)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "revenant-c07",
      "source_id": "revenant-s2",
      "quote": "No folclore, um revenant ou revenã é um espírito ou cadáver animado, em que se acredita ter sido revivido da morte para assombrar os vivos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Portugis juga menyebut revenant roh atau mayat hidup yang dipercaya bangkit dari kematian untuk menghantui orang hidup.",
        "en": "The Portuguese article also calls the revenant a spirit or animated corpse believed revived from death to haunt the living."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "revenant-c08",
      "source_id": "revenant-s3",
      "quote": "Revenant je v mytologii a folklóru oživené mrtvé tělo, které přichází do kontaktu s živými. Slovo je odvozeno od latinského revenire („navracet se“)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Ceko, revenant adalah jasad mati yang hidup kembali dan berhubungan dengan orang hidup, dan katanya dari bahasa Latin revenire, \"kembali\".",
        "en": "According to the Czech article, a revenant is a revived dead body that comes into contact with the living, the word from Latin revenire, 'to return'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "revenant-c09",
      "source_id": "revenant-s3",
      "quote": "Přesvědčení, že neobvyklá smrt je příčinou posmrtného bloudění, je u Indoevropanů obecně rozšířena.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Keyakinan bahwa kematian yang tidak wajar menyebabkan arwah mengembara setelah mati tersebar luas di kalangan bangsa Indo-Eropa.",
        "en": "The belief that an unusual death causes wandering after death is widespread among Indo-Europeans."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Asal kata",
        "en": "Origin of the word"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Dari bahasa Prancis Kuno revenant.",
            "en": "From Old French revenant."
          },
          "claim_ids": [
            "revenant-c01"
          ]
        },
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Dari bahasa Latin revenire.",
            "en": "From Latin revenire."
          },
          "claim_ids": [
            "revenant-c08"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia en, pt, dan cs; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",

    }
  ]
}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Squasc adalah makhluk kecil berbulu mirip tupai berwajah manusia dari cerita rakyat Lombardia timur, dipakai untuk menakuti anak dan suka mengerjai gadis-gadis.",
    "en": "The squasc is a small hairy squirrel-like being with a human face from eastern Lombardy folklore, used to scare children and fond of pranking girls.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "squasc-c01",
      "squasc-c02",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Squasc adalah makhluk mitologis Lombardia timur, kecil, berbulu cokelat kemerahan, mirip tupai tanpa ekor tetapi berwajah manusia. Perannya di antara roh jahat penakut anak seperti uomo nero dan peri usil yang suka berbuat lelucon kasar, terutama kepada gadis-gadis. Ia juga disebut Sanguanel, Sgranf, Farfarel, atau Ana Sosana.",
      "en": "The squasc is a mythological being of eastern Lombardy, small, tawny and hairy, like a tailless squirrel but with a human face. Its role lies between a child-scaring evil spirit like the uomo nero and a sprite fond of rough pranks, especially on girls. It is also called Sanguanel, Sgranf, Farfarel or Ana Sosana.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "squasc-c01",
      "source_id": "squasc-s1",
      "quote": "The squasc (pronounced [ˈskwaʃ]) is a mythological being of the Eastern Lombardy region folklore.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Squasc adalah makhluk mitologis dalam cerita rakyat Lombardia timur.",
        "en": "The squasc is a mythological being of eastern Lombardy folklore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "squasc-c02",
      "source_id": "squasc-s2",
      "quote": "Si dice sia un essere piccolo, peloso, fulvo, simile ad uno scoiattolo senza coda ma con volto antropomorfo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Italia, ia makhluk kecil berbulu cokelat kemerahan, mirip tupai tanpa ekor tetapi berwajah manusia.",
        "en": "According to the Italian article it is a small, hairy, tawny being like a tailless squirrel but with a human face."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "squasc-c03",
      "source_id": "squasc-s2",
      "quote": "La sua funzione si trova a metà tra quella di uno spirito cattivo (assimilabile all'uomo nero) e un folletto. Come il primo, infatti, è chiamato in causa per spaventare i bambini, mentre come il secondo ama fare scherzi anche pesanti ad ogni categoria di persone, con una certa predilezione per le fanciulle.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Perannya di antara roh jahat seperti uomo nero dan peri usil: ia dipakai untuk menakuti anak, tetapi juga suka berbuat lelucon kasar kepada siapa saja, terutama gadis-gadis.",
        "en": "Its role lies between an evil spirit like the uomo nero and a sprite: it is invoked to frighten children, but also loves playing rough jokes on anyone, especially girls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "squasc-c04",
      "source_id": "squasc-s2",
      "quote": "È conosciuto anche con le varianti di Sanguanel, Sgranf, Farfarel, Ana Sosana.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga dikenal dengan nama Sanguanel, Sgranf, Farfarel, dan Ana Sosana.",
        "en": "It is also known as Sanguanel, Sgranf, Farfarel and Ana Sosana."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "squasc-c05",
      "source_id": "squasc-s3",
      "quote": "El Squasc a l'è un vesser mitologich de la tradizzion de la Lombardia oriental, piscinin, compagn de 'n sghirat ma cont el vis de om.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Lombard juga menyebut squasc makhluk mitologis kecil dari tradisi Lombardia timur, mirip tupai tetapi berwajah manusia.",
        "en": "The Lombard article also calls the squasc a small mythological being of eastern Lombard tradition, like a squirrel but with a human face."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Tante Arie adalah peri baik dari Montbéliard dan Jura yang membawa hadiah Natal dengan keledainya kepada anak-anak yang patuh, konon penjelmaan Countess Henriette.",
    "en": "Tante Arie is a kindly fairy of Montbéliard and the Jura who brings Christmas presents on her donkey to good children, said to be a reincarnation of Countess Henriette.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "tante-arie-c01",
      "tante-arie-c02",
      "tante-arie-c07"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Tante Arie adalah peri baik dari cerita rakyat Montbéliard, Franche-Comté, dan Jura Swiss, yang menurut legenda penjelmaan Countess Henriette dari Montbéliard. Pada malam 24 Desember ia turun dari gunung dengan keledainya membawa hadiah bagi anak-anak yang patuh, sehingga ia semacam \"Ibu Natal\" seperti Befana dan lebih tua daripada Père Noël di daerah itu.",
      "en": "Tante Arie is a kindly fairy of the folklore of Montbéliard, Franche-Comté and the Swiss Jura, said to be a reincarnation of Countess Henriette of Montbéliard. On the evening of 24 December she comes down from the mountains with her donkey bringing presents for good children, a sort of 'Mother Christmas' like the Befana and older than Father Christmas in the region.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "tante-arie-c01",
        "tante-arie-c02",
        "tante-arie-c04",
        "tante-arie-c05",
        "tante-arie-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dengan berbagai penyamaran ia meminta tumpangan untuk menguji dan mendorong rumah tangga yang baik, membantu gadis rajin memintal, memberkati panen, dan melindungi gandum dari badai. Ia kadang dikatakan berkaki angsa dan dapat menjadi vouivre, naga berkaki dua.",
      "en": "In various disguises she asks for shelter to test and encourage good households, helps diligent girls spin, blesses the harvest and shields the grain from storms. She is sometimes said to have goose feet and to turn into the vouivre, a two-legged dragon.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "tante-arie-c03",
        "tante-arie-c06",
        "tante-arie-c08",
        "tante-arie-c09"
      ]
    }

      "quote": "Tante Arie (ou Tainte Airie en franc-comtois) est une bonne fée du folklore des environs de Montbéliard. Elle visite les enfants sages ou non à Noël et elle est antérieure au père Noël et Saint-Nicolas qui n’existe pas dans l'ancien comté de Montbéliard.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tante Arie (Tainte Airie dalam bahasa Franche-Comté) adalah peri baik dari cerita rakyat sekitar Montbéliard yang mengunjungi anak-anak saat Natal, lebih tua daripada Sinterklas dan Père Noël di bekas kabupaten Montbéliard.",
        "en": "Tante Arie (Tainte Airie in Franc-Comtois) is a good fairy of the folklore around Montbéliard who visits children at Christmas, older than Father Christmas and Saint Nicholas in the former county of Montbéliard."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tante-arie-c02",
      "source_id": "tante-arie-s1",
      "quote": "La tante Arie est, selon la légende, une réincarnation de la bienfaitrice et regrettée comtesse Henriette de Montbéliard (1387-1444).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, Tante Arie adalah penjelmaan kembali Countess Henriette dari Montbéliard (1387-1444) yang dermawan dan dikenang.",
        "en": "By legend Tante Arie is a reincarnation of the beloved, generous Countess Henriette of Montbéliard (1387-1444)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tante-arie-c03",
      "source_id": "tante-arie-s1",
      "quote": "Sous des déguisements divers, elle demande souvent l’hospitalité pour connaître les gens et encourager les ménages vertueux, travailleurs, soigneux et charitables. Elle apparaît notamment au marché de Noël de Montbéliard.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dengan berbagai penyamaran ia meminta tumpangan untuk mengenal orang dan mendorong rumah tangga yang saleh, rajin, rapi, dan dermawan; ia muncul di pasar Natal Montbéliard.",
        "en": "In various disguises she asks for hospitality to get to know people and encourage virtuous, hard-working, tidy and charitable households; she appears at the Montbéliard Christmas market."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tante-arie-c04",
      "source_id": "tante-arie-s1",
      "quote": "Tante Arie est, à l'instar de la fée Befana italienne, un exemple de substitut féminin au père Noël, une sorte de « mère Noël ».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seperti peri Befana dari Italia, Tante Arie adalah contoh pengganti perempuan bagi Père Noël, semacam \"Ibu Natal\".",
        "en": "Like the Italian Befana, Tante Arie is an example of a female stand-in for Father Christmas, a kind of 'Mother Christmas'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tante-arie-c05",
      "source_id": "tante-arie-s2",
      "quote": "Тётушка Ари известна в фольклоре восточной Франции (Франш-Конте и Бургундии) и Швейцарии (кантон Юра), где считается доброй феей, покровительствующей людям.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Rusia, Tante Arie dikenal dalam cerita rakyat Prancis timur (Franche-Comté dan Burgundia) dan Swiss (kanton Jura) sebagai peri baik pelindung manusia.",
        "en": "According to the Russian article, Tante Arie is known in the folklore of eastern France (Franche-Comté and Burgundy) and Switzerland (canton of Jura) as a kindly fairy protecting people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tante-arie-c06",
      "source_id": "tante-arie-s2",
      "quote": "в частности, иногда утверждается, что у неё гусиные лапы — об этом говорят следы, которые она оставила на золе, рассыпанной местными жителями близ её жилища. Кроме того, некоторые легенды говорят о её способности превращаться в другое существо из французской мифологии — двуногого дракона вуивра.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang ia dikatakan berkaki angsa, dibuktikan jejaknya di abu yang ditaburkan warga dekat rumahnya, dan sebagian legenda menyebut ia dapat berubah menjadi vouivre, naga berkaki dua.",
        "en": "She is sometimes said to have goose feet, shown by the tracks she left in ashes scattered by locals near her home, and some legends say she can turn into the vouivre, a two-legged dragon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tante-arie-c07",
      "source_id": "tante-arie-s2",
      "quote": "Вечером 24 декабря она покидает горы и, нагрузив ослика подарками и лакомствами, везёт их детям, которые в течение года были послушными.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada malam 24 Desember ia turun dari gunung dengan keledai bermuatan hadiah dan penganan untuk anak-anak yang patuh sepanjang tahun.",
        "en": "On the evening of 24 December she leaves the mountains with her donkey loaded with presents and sweets for children who were good all year."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tante-arie-c08",
      "source_id": "tante-arie-s2",
      "quote": "Сама она искусная прядильщица, поэтому охотно помогает прилежным девушкам прясть, однако нерадивой девице может, напротив, спутать пряжу",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia pemintal ulung yang senang membantu gadis rajin memintal, tetapi dapat mengusutkan benang gadis yang malas.",
        "en": "A skilled spinner, she gladly helps diligent girls spin but may tangle a lazy girl's yarn."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tante-arie-c09",
      "source_id": "tante-arie-s3",
      "quote": "Ze wordt vereerd in het Juragebergte en zegent de oogst en beschermt het graan tegen vorst en stormen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Belanda, ia dihormati di Pegunungan Jura, memberkati panen, dan melindungi gandum dari embun beku dan badai.",
        "en": "According to the Dutch article she is honoured in the Jura mountains, blessing the harvest and protecting the grain from frost and storms."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {

    "id": "Tarand atau parandrus adalah binatang legendaris sebesar lembu dengan kepala dan tanduk rusa yang dapat mengubah warna bulunya seperti bunglon, dari tulisan kuno dan bestiari.",
    "en": "The tarand or parandrus is a legendary ox-sized beast with a deer's head and antlers that changes its fur colour like a chameleon, from ancient writings and bestiaries.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Tarand, tarandus, atau parandrus adalah binatang legendaris mirip rusa kutub atau rusa besar, sebesar lembu dengan kepala rusa dan tanduk bercabang, yang mengubah warna bulunya menyerupai pohon dan tanah sehingga sulit ditangkap. Teks yang dikaitkan dengan Aristoteles, Plinius, dan Aelianus menempatkannya di Skithia, sedangkan Solinus di Aethiopia.",
      "en": "The tarand, tarandus or parandrus is a legendary reindeer- or moose-like beast, ox-sized with a deer's head and branching antlers, that changes its fur colour to match trees and ground, so it is hard to catch. Texts ascribed to Aristotle, Pliny and Aelian place it in Scythia, while Solinus puts it in Aethiopia.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "tarand-animal-c01",
        "tarand-animal-c03",
        "tarand-animal-c04",
        "tarand-animal-c05",
        "tarand-animal-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia muncul lagi dalam York Mystery Cycle (1440) dan Pantagruel karya Rabelais (1552), kebenarannya dibahas Baron Cuvier, dan nama ilmiah rusa kutub, Rangifer tarandus, berasal darinya. Uraian \"Aristoteles\" berasal dari karya yang keliru dikaitkan kepadanya.",
      "en": "It reappears in the York Mystery Cycle (1440) and Rabelais's Pantagruel (1552), Baron Cuvier discussed whether it was real, and the reindeer's scientific name, Rangifer tarandus, comes from it. The 'Aristotle' account comes from a work wrongly attributed to him.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "tarand-animal-c01",
      "source_id": "tarand-animal-s1",
      "quote": "A tarand, also known as a tarandos, tarandus, parandrus, or parandros, is a legendary reindeer/moose-like creature with chameleon properties.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tarand, juga tarandos, tarandus, parandrus, atau parandros, adalah makhluk legendaris mirip rusa kutub atau rusa besar dengan kemampuan seperti bunglon.",
        "en": "A tarand, also tarandos, tarandus, parandrus or parandros, is a legendary reindeer- or moose-like creature with chameleon-like powers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tarand-animal-c02",
      "source_id": "tarand-animal-s1",
      "quote": "It was also mentioned in Pliny's History of the Animals (Tarandus), Aelian's De Natura Animalium (Tarandos), Solinus (Parandrus) and Caesar, appearing again in key texts of the medieval period, such as  The York Mystery Cycle (1440) and Francois Rabelais' Pantagruel (1552).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia disebut Plinius, Aelianus dalam De Natura Animalium, Solinus, dan Caesar, lalu muncul lagi dalam teks abad pertengahan seperti York Mystery Cycle (1440) dan Pantagruel karya François Rabelais (1552).",
        "en": "It is mentioned by Pliny, Aelian in De Natura Animalium, Solinus and Caesar, and appears again in later texts such as the York Mystery Cycle (1440) and Rabelais's Pantagruel (1552)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tarand-animal-c03",
      "source_id": "tarand-animal-s1",
      "quote": "Aristotle, Pliny and Aelian write that the animal (Tarandus) was living in Scythia, while Solinus write that the animal that he describes (Parandus) was living in Aethiopia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aristoteles, Plinius, dan Aelianus menulis bahwa hewan ini hidup di Skithia, sedangkan Solinus menempatkannya di Aethiopia.",
        "en": "Aristotle, Pliny and Aelian place the animal in Scythia, while Solinus puts it in Aethiopia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tarand-animal-c04",
      "source_id": "tarand-animal-s1",
      "quote": "Among the Scythians called Geloni they say that there is a beast, excessively rare, which is called tarandus; they say that it changes the colour of its hair according to the place it is in. For this reason it is difficult to catch; for it becomes the same colour as the trees and the ground",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut teks Aristotelian, di kalangan suku Skithia Geloni ada binatang sangat langka bernama tarandus yang mengubah warna bulunya sesuai tempatnya, sehingga sulit ditangkap karena menyerupai warna pohon dan tanah.",
        "en": "According to the Aristotelian text, among the Scythian Geloni there is a very rare beast called tarandus that changes the colour of its hair to suit its surroundings, so it is hard to catch, taking the colour of trees and ground."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tarand-animal-c05",
      "source_id": "tarand-animal-s1",
      "quote": "But this animal is of the size of an ox. But its head is of the same kind as a deer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Binatang itu sebesar lembu dengan kepala seperti rusa.",
        "en": "The animal is the size of an ox, with a deer-like head."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tarand-animal-c06",
      "source_id": "tarand-animal-s1",
      "quote": "The veracity of the tarand was discussed by Jean Léopold Nicolas Frédéric, Baron Cuvier (1769–1832).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kebenaran keberadaan tarand dibahas oleh Baron Cuvier (1769–1832).",
        "en": "The truth of the tarand was discussed by Baron Cuvier (1769–1832)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tarand-animal-c07",
      "source_id": "tarand-animal-s2",
      "quote": "Es hat die Größe eines Ochsen, (teils langes) Fell, gespaltene Hufe und ein Geweih. Es kann, um sich zu tarnen, seine Fellfarbe ändern.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, ia sebesar lembu, berbulu (kadang panjang), berkuku belah, bertanduk cabang, dan dapat mengubah warna bulunya untuk menyamar.",
        "en": "According to the German article it is ox-sized, furred (sometimes long-haired), cloven-hoofed and antlered, and can change its fur colour to camouflage itself."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tarand-animal-c08",
      "source_id": "tarand-animal-s2",
      "quote": "Der wissenschaftliche Name des Rentiers (Rangifer tarandus) ist von seinem Namen abgeleitet.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama ilmiah rusa kutub, Rangifer tarandus, berasal dari namanya.",
        "en": "The reindeer's scientific name, Rangifer tarandus, comes from its name."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tarand-animal-c09",
      "source_id": "tarand-animal-s3",
      "quote": "De mirabilibus auscultationibus (Sur les choses merveilleuses entendues) (en), ouvrage faussement attribué à Aristote, en fournit une description",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, deskripsi \"Aristoteles\" itu berasal dari De mirabilibus auscultationibus, karya yang keliru dikaitkan dengan Aristoteles.",
        "en": "According to the French article, the 'Aristotle' description comes from De mirabilibus auscultationibus, a work falsely attributed to Aristotle."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Wulver adalah makhluk berkepala serigala dari cerita rakyat Shetland yang baik hati, suka memancing, dan meninggalkan ikan bagi orang miskin, meski asal-usulnya diperdebatkan.",
    "en": "The wulver is a kindly wolf-headed being of Shetland folklore who loves fishing and leaves fish for the poor, though its origin is disputed.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wulver-c01",
      "wulver-c03",
      "wulver-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Wulver adalah makhluk Kepulauan Shetland berwujud lelaki berkepala serigala dan berbulu cokelat pendek, yang tinggal di gua di lereng bukit. Menurut Jessie Saxby ia baik hati, ramah kepada warga, dan memancing berjam-jam dari batu yang disebut Wulver's Stane, lalu meninggalkan ikan di jendela orang miskin; kisah lain menyebutnya buas bila diusik.",
      "en": "The wulver is a being of the Shetland Islands, a man with a wolf's head and short brown hair who lives in a cave on a hillside. According to Jessie Saxby it is kindly and friendly to locals, fishing for hours from a rock called the Wulver's Stane and leaving fish on poor people's windowsills; other accounts say it turns violent if provoked.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wulver-c01",
        "wulver-c02",
        "wulver-c03",
        "wulver-c04",
        "wulver-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Asal-usulnya diperdebatkan: Susan Schoon Eberly menduga kisah ini berasal dari orang dengan penyakit seperti sindrom Hunter, sedangkan Brian Smith dari Shetland Museum menganggapnya rekaan Saxby sendiri.",
      "en": "Its origin is disputed: Susan Schoon Eberly suggests the tale arose from people with conditions such as Hunter syndrome, while Brian Smith of the Shetland Museum considers it Saxby's own invention.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wulver-c05",
        "wulver-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "wulver-c01",
      "source_id": "wulver-s1",
      "quote": "The wulver or wullver is a kind of wolf-like humanoid creature in the folklore of the Shetland Islands of Scotland.  In modern times, the origin of the wulver has been disputed.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wulver atau wullver adalah makhluk mirip manusia berwujud serigala dalam cerita rakyat Kepulauan Shetland, Skotlandia, yang asal-usulnya kini diperdebatkan.",
        "en": "The wulver or wullver is a wolf-like humanoid of the folklore of the Shetland Islands of Scotland, whose origin is now disputed."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wulver-c02",
      "source_id": "wulver-s1",
      "quote": "The wulver is said by the Shetland folklorist Jessie Saxby to be benevolent, although later accounts state that they became violent if provoked. They were generally friendly to locals, however, and were known to share the fish they caught with them.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut folkloris Shetland Jessie Saxby, wulver baik hati, meski kisah lain menyebutnya menjadi buas bila diusik; mereka umumnya ramah kepada warga dan berbagi ikan tangkapannya.",
        "en": "The Shetland folklorist Jessie Saxby calls the wulver benevolent, though later accounts say it turned violent if provoked; wulvers were usually friendly to locals and shared the fish they caught."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wulver-c03",
      "source_id": "wulver-s1",
      "quote": "The Wulver was a creature like a man with a wolf's head. He had short brown hair all over him. His home was a cave dug out of the side of a steep knowe, half-way up a hill.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saxby menulis bahwa wulver seperti lelaki berkepala serigala, berbulu cokelat pendek di seluruh tubuh, dan tinggal di gua di lereng bukit yang curam.",
        "en": "Saxby wrote that the wulver was like a man with a wolf's head, covered in short brown hair, living in a cave dug into a steep knoll halfway up a hill."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wulver-c04",
      "source_id": "wulver-s1",
      "quote": "He was fond of catching and eating fish, and had a small rock in the deep water which is known to this day as the \"Wulver's Stane\". There he would sit fishing sillaks and piltaks for hour after hour. He was reported to have frequently left a few fish on the window-sill of some poor body.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia suka menangkap dan memakan ikan dari batu kecil di air dalam yang sampai kini disebut Wulver's Stane, dan sering meninggalkan beberapa ekor ikan di ambang jendela orang miskin.",
        "en": "He liked catching and eating fish from a little rock in deep water still called the Wulver's Stane, and often left a few fish on a poor person's windowsill."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wulver-c05",
      "source_id": "wulver-s1",
      "quote": "Susan Schoon Eberly, has speculated that the tale of the wulver may have its basis in humans suffering a medical condition; possibly Hunter syndrome or lycanthropy, she suggests.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Susan Schoon Eberly menduga kisah wulver mungkin berasal dari manusia yang menderita penyakit, mungkin sindrom Hunter atau likantropi.",
        "en": "Susan Schoon Eberly has speculated the wulver tale may come from people with a medical condition, perhaps Hunter syndrome or lycanthropy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wulver-c06",
      "source_id": "wulver-s1",
      "quote": "Others, such as the Shetland Museum archivist Brian Smith, argue that the wulver is an entirely fictitious creation that was never part of Shetland folklore, contending the creature is solely the creation of Saxby.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Yang lain, seperti arsiparis Shetland Museum Brian Smith, berpendapat wulver sepenuhnya rekaan Saxby dan tidak pernah menjadi bagian cerita rakyat Shetland.",
        "en": "Others, such as the Shetland Museum archivist Brian Smith, argue the wulver is wholly Saxby's invention and was never part of Shetland folklore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wulver-c07",
      "source_id": "wulver-s2",
      "quote": "El wulver és una espècie de criatura de llop humanoide que forma part del folklore de les illes Shetland de la costa d'Escòcia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Katalan juga menyebut wulver makhluk serigala mirip manusia dari cerita rakyat Kepulauan Shetland di lepas pantai Skotlandia.",
        "en": "The Catalan article also calls the wulver a humanoid wolf creature of the folklore of the Shetland Islands off Scotland."
      }
    }
  ],

    "en": "Basandere, 'lady of the forest', is a hairy wild woman of Basque mythology, wife of Basajaun, who combs her hair with a golden comb at cave mouths while guarding treasure.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Basandere, \"nyonya hutan\" atau \"nyonya liar\", adalah istri Basajaun dalam mitologi Basque, perempuan liar berbulu dan berambut sangat panjang yang tinggal di Pyrenees Basque, terutama hutan Iraty. Ia biasa berada dekat mulut gua atau mata air, menyisir rambut dengan sisir emas sambil mengawasi hartanya, misalnya di gua puncak Gunung Mondarrain; ia lenyap bila didekati.",
      "en": "Basandere, 'lady of the forest' or 'wild lady', is Basajaun's wife in Basque mythology, a hairy wild woman with very long hair living in the Basque Pyrenees, especially the forest of Iraty. She sits by cave mouths or springs combing her hair with a golden comb and watching her treasure, as at the cave on top of Mount Mondarrain; she vanishes when approached.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "basandere-c01",
      "source_id": "basandere-s1",
      "quote": "Basandere, Basaandere, Basa Andere, Basandere (prononcer « bachandéré ») désigne dans la mythologie basque une créature imaginaire, un jentil, femme de Basajaun. C'est une sorte de femme poilue et sauvage, vivant dans les Pyrénées basques, en particulier dans la forêt d'Iraty.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Basandere atau Basa Andere dalam mitologi Basque adalah makhluk khayalan, seorang jentil, istri Basajaun, perempuan liar berbulu yang tinggal di Pyrenees Basque, terutama hutan Iraty.",
        "en": "Basandere or Basa Andere in Basque mythology is an imaginary being, a jentil, the wife of Basajaun, a hairy wild woman living in the Basque Pyrenees, especially the forest of Iraty."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basandere-c02",
      "source_id": "basandere-s1",
      "quote": "En basque, Basandere signifie littéralement « la dame de la forêt » ou « la dame sauvage ».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bahasa Basque, Basandere berarti \"nyonya hutan\" atau \"nyonya liar\".",
        "en": "In Basque, Basandere literally means 'lady of the forest' or 'wild lady'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basandere-c03",
      "source_id": "basandere-s1",
      "quote": "Les Basandere, compagnes des Basajaunak, ont pour habitude de rester à proximité de l'entrée des cavernes, ou près des fontaines. Elles y passent des heures à peigner leur chevelure, tout en gardant un œil sur leurs vastes trésors. Le vol d'un peigne d'or, par un berger, est un thème de conte fréquent.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Basandere, pasangan para Basajaun, biasa berada dekat mulut gua atau mata air, berjam-jam menyisir rambut sambil mengawasi harta mereka; pencurian sisir emas oleh gembala adalah tema dongeng yang sering muncul.",
        "en": "The Basandere, companions of the Basajaunak, stay near cave mouths or springs, combing their hair for hours while watching over their great treasures; a shepherd stealing a golden comb is a common tale theme."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basandere-c04",
      "source_id": "basandere-s1",
      "quote": "Il est pratiquement impossible de voir de près l'une de ces créatures, car dès que l'on s'en approche, elles disparaissent comme par magie.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hampir mustahil melihat mereka dari dekat karena mereka lenyap seperti sihir begitu didekati.",
        "en": "It is almost impossible to see one up close, since they vanish as if by magic when approached."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basandere-c05",
      "source_id": "basandere-s1",
      "quote": "Par ses caractères physiques et son comportement, elle s'apparente aux « femmes sauvages » ou sylvains, protecteurs de la nature et des activités agro-pastorales",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ciri fisik dan perilakunya mirip \"perempuan liar\" atau roh hutan, pelindung alam serta kegiatan pertanian dan penggembalaan.",
        "en": "In body and behaviour she resembles the 'wild women' or sylvans, protectors of nature and of farming and herding."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basandere-c06",
      "source_id": "basandere-s2",
      "quote": "Según la leyenda, a la entrada de la cueva situada en la cima del monte Mondarrain una basandere suele peinar su melena con un peine de oro.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, di mulut gua di puncak Gunung Mondarrain seorang basandere biasa menyisir rambutnya dengan sisir emas.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, at the mouth of the cave on the summit of Mount Mondarrain a basandere combs her hair with a golden comb."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basandere-c07",
      "source_id": "basandere-s3",
      "quote": "La Basandere ('senyora del bosc, senyora salvatge', del basc basa 'bosc, salvatge' i andere 'senyora') és un personatge de la mitologia basca amb forma humana, el cos cobert de pèl i una cabellera molt llarga.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Katalan, Basandere (dari basa, \"hutan, liar\", dan andere, \"nyonya\") berwujud manusia, bertubuh berbulu, dan berambut sangat panjang.",
        "en": "According to the Catalan article, the Basandere (from basa, 'forest, wild', and andere, 'lady') is humanlike, with a hairy body and very long hair."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basandere-c08",
      "source_id": "basandere-s3",
      "quote": "una característica que remet a la deessa Mari i, sobretot, al personatge de l'Encantada.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kebiasaan menyisir rambut sambil menjaga harta itu mengingatkan pada dewi Mari dan terutama tokoh Encantada.",
        "en": "This hair-combing over treasure recalls the goddess Mari and especially the figure of the Encantada."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Behigorri, \"sapi merah\", adalah roh gua mitologi Basque berwujud sapi atau banteng merah yang menjaga gua Mari dan hartanya serta menyerang orang yang mendekat.",
    "en": "Behigorri, 'red cow', is a Basque cave spirit in the form of a red cow or bull that guards Mari's caves and treasures and attacks those who come near.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "behigorri-c01",
      "behigorri-c02",
      "behigorri-c07",
      "behigorri-c08"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Behigorri atau Beigorri, \"sapi merah\", adalah roh yang tinggal di gua dan rongga bawah tanah, keluar sebagai sapi merah, atau di daerah lain sebagai banteng merah Zezengorri; anaknya Aatxe juga roh serupa. Ia mirip sapi betizu, menjaga rumah Mari dan harta tersembunyi, dan menyerang orang yang mendekati tempat sucinya; gua Marizulo di Larrunarri dikatakan dihuni sapi merah.",
      "en": "Behigorri or Beigorri, 'red cow', is a spirit of caves and underground hollows that comes out as a red cow, or elsewhere as the red bull Zezengorri; its calf Aatxe is a similar spirit. It looks like betizu cattle, guards Mari's homes and hidden treasure, and attacks those who approach its sanctuary; the Marizulo cave on Larrunarri is said to house a red cow.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "behigorri-c01",
        "behigorri-c02",
        "behigorri-c03",
        "behigorri-c05",
        "behigorri-c07",
        "behigorri-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Roh semacam ini sering terkait gua bergambar hewan dari zaman Paleolitik, dan prasasti Romawi di Pyrenees Gascogne menyebut dewa seperti Baicorrix yang mungkin setara dengannya.",
      "en": "Such spirits are often tied to caves with Palaeolithic animal paintings, and Roman inscriptions in the Gascon Pyrenees name deities like Baicorrix that may be comparable.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "behigorri-c04",
        "behigorri-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "behigorri-c01",
      "source_id": "behigorri-s1",
      "quote": "Dans la mythologie basque, Behigorri ou Beigorri (« vache rouge » en basque) est une entité qui vit dans les grottes ou autres cavités souterraines.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Basque, Behigorri atau Beigorri (\"sapi merah\") adalah makhluk yang tinggal di gua atau rongga bawah tanah.",
        "en": "In Basque mythology Behigorri or Beigorri ('red cow') is a being that lives in caves or other underground cavities."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "behigorri-c02",
      "source_id": "behigorri-s2",
      "quote": "és un geni que viu en coves o altres cavitats subterrànies i que, en sortir-ne, adopta la forma de vaca vermella. Segons la zona o la llegenda, en comptes d'una vaca és un toro vermell i aleshores se l'anomena Zezengorri (zezen és 'toro' en basc).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Katalan, roh ini tinggal di gua dan berwujud sapi merah saat keluar; di daerah lain ia banteng merah yang disebut Zezengorri.",
        "en": "According to the Catalan article this spirit lives in caves and takes the form of a red cow when it comes out; elsewhere it is a red bull called Zezengorri."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "behigorri-c03",
      "source_id": "behigorri-s1",
      "quote": "Son petit, Aatxe qui signifie veau en basque est aussi appelé Behigorri, Aatxegorri ou Aatexegorrila, Txaalgorri, Txalgorri ou Txahalgorri.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Anaknya, Aatxe (\"anak sapi\"), juga disebut Behigorri, Aatxegorri, Txaalgorri, atau Txahalgorri.",
        "en": "Its calf, Aatxe ('calf'), is also called Behigorri, Aatxegorri, Txaalgorri or Txahalgorri."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "behigorri-c04",
      "source_id": "behigorri-s1",
      "quote": "De récentes études ont démontré que ces entités ou génies, Behigorri, Laminak et Ihizi, sont souvent rattachés à des lieux où ont été représentés ces mêmes animaux par les hommes du Paléolithique.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penelitian terbaru menunjukkan Behigorri, Laminak, dan Ihizi sering terkait tempat yang memuat gambar hewan yang sama buatan manusia Paleolitik.",
        "en": "Recent studies show Behigorri, the Laminak and Ihizi are often tied to places where Palaeolithic people depicted the same animals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "behigorri-c05",
      "source_id": "behigorri-s2",
      "quote": "A la cova de Marizulo (literalment, 'forat de Mari') del mont Larrunarri, prop d'Amezketa, hi viu una vaca vermella.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di gua Marizulo (\"lubang Mari\") di Gunung Larrunarri dekat Amezketa tinggal seekor sapi merah.",
        "en": "A red cow lives in the Marizulo cave ('Mari's hole') on Mount Larrunarri near Amezketa."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "behigorri-c06",
      "source_id": "behigorri-s2",
      "quote": "Als Pirineus de Gascunya, antic territori dels aquitans, s'hi han trobat nombroses inscripcions d'època romana amb noms que semblen referir-se a alguna mena de divinitat equiparable a Behigorri: Baicorrix, Baigorisco, Baigorix",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Pyrenees Gascogne, bekas wilayah Aquitani, ditemukan banyak prasasti zaman Romawi dengan nama yang tampaknya merujuk dewa setara Behigorri, seperti Baicorrix dan Baigorix.",
        "en": "In the Gascon Pyrenees, old Aquitanian territory, many Roman-era inscriptions bear names that seem to refer to a deity comparable to Behigorri, such as Baicorrix and Baigorix."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "behigorri-c07",
      "source_id": "behigorri-s3",
      "quote": "Kolore gorriko behi, idi, txahal, txekor edo zezenaren itxura hartzen omen du. Batzuetan handik ateratzen da bere santutegiak defendatzeko bertara sartu nahian diren edota, besterik gabe, hurbiltzen diren pertsonei erasotuz.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Basque, ia berwujud sapi, lembu, anak sapi, atau banteng merah, dan kadang keluar untuk menyerang orang yang ingin memasuki atau sekadar mendekati tempat sucinya.",
        "en": "According to the Basque article it takes the form of a red cow, ox, calf or bull, and sometimes comes out to attack people who try to enter or simply approach its sanctuary."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "behigorri-c08",
      "source_id": "behigorri-s3",
      "quote": "Itxuraz Euskal Herriko mendietan bizi diren behi betizuen antzekoa da, eta Mariren etxeak edota jendearen gogoan belaunaldiz belaunaldi gordetako eta lur azpian ezkutatutako altxor mitikoak zaintzen omen ditu.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rupanya mirip sapi betizu yang hidup di pegunungan Basque, dan ia menjaga rumah Mari serta harta mitis yang tersembunyi di bawah tanah.",
        "en": "It looks like the betizu cattle of the Basque mountains and guards Mari's homes and mythical treasures hidden underground."
      }
    }
  ],

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cheval Gauvin adalah kuda jahat legendaris Franche-Comté dan Jura Swiss yang berkeliaran di sepanjang sungai, hutan, dan kuburan, lalu menenggelamkan penunggangnya atau melemparkannya ke jurang. Di Swiss ia menculik gadis muda, dan satu legenda menjadikannya tunggangan Amauri III de Joux. Ia pertanda kematian dan momok penakut anak, mungkin jelmaan peri usil.",
      "en": "The Cheval Gauvin is a legendary evil horse of Franche-Comté and the Swiss Jura that roams along streams, forests and graveyards and drowns its riders or flings them into chasms. In Switzerland it carries off young girls, and one legend makes it the mount of Amauri III de Joux. It is an omen of death and a children's bogey, perhaps a transformed goblin.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cheval-gauvin-c01",
        "cheval-gauvin-c03",
        "cheval-gauvin-c04",

    {
      "id": "Désiré Monnier menerbitkan legendanya dari Chamblay pada 1854, tempat seorang perempuan mengaku bertemu kuda itu di kuburan. Namanya sama dengan ksatria Gauvain, dan Jean-Louis Thouard menggambarkannya pada 1996.",
      "en": "Désiré Monnier published its legend from Chamblay in 1854, where a woman claimed to have met it in the cemetery. Its name is that of the knight Gawain, and Jean-Louis Thouard illustrated it in 1996.",

      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {

        "en": "The Cheval Gauvin ('Gauvin horse') is a legendary evil horse of Franche-Comté, France, and the Jura Mountains of Switzerland, which wanders along streams, through forests and graveyards and tries to kill its riders by drowning them or throwing them into an abyss."

    {

      "id": "cheval-gauvin-c03",
      "source_id": "cheval-gauvin-s1",
      "quote": "In Switzerland, the horse gallops through villages and abducts young girls. Several legends are attached to it, one of which makes it the mount of the medieval lord Amauri III de Joux.",
